# A066 / B14 — restriction-compatible heat dissipation

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every Boolean sign f on a finite uniform cube, retain K(f)=integral_0^infinity J_f(t)dt=(H_nat(f)-b(f))/2, with b(f)=p_empty ln(1/p_empty). All new charges use natural logarithms; divide entropy by ln(2) for bits. B14 lead v2 requests an exact physical-block heat drop and comparison with C006 on majority and point-defected addressing, explicitly accounting for level mass.

**Outcome:** an exact positive physical-restriction ledger is proved, with its singleton channel and level charges paid uniformly by queried influence. The remaining nonempty-context conditional erasure has exactly the same optimal universal constant as C018's full erasure. Majority and point-defected addressing are computed analytically and checked exactly in frozen finite domains. No universal residual payment or full FEI proof is supplied.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

The complete proofs are saved in `DERIVATION.md`. For a physical block B, residual spectral index T, and assignment y, let p_y(T)=hat(f_y)(T)^2 and q_T=E_y p_y(T)=sum_{U subset B}p_{U union T}. Define

M_T=sum_U p_{U union T}ln(q_T/p_{U union T}),
C_T=E_y p_y(T)ln(p_y(T)/q_T).

The usual C006 entropy decrement is M_B+C_B, summing all rows. Remove the empty residual row to obtain M_B^+, C_B^+, and set ell_B=sum_{nonempty U subset B}p_U ln(1/p_U). The exact new heat identity is

2[K(f)-E_y K(f_y)]=M_B^+ + C_B^+ + ell_B.

All three terms are nonnegative, so K decreases in expectation under every physical restriction. This accounts for the constant-mode boundary exactly: ell_B is the information in Fourier coefficients supported wholly inside the erased block, which become constants in the children.

For B={i}, the channel is at most 2 Inf_i(f) nats. Booleanity gives |hat(f)({i})|<=Inf_i(f), hence ell_{ {i} }<= (2/e)Inf_i(f). Both charges are paid using the same queried coordinate; in a stopping tree their cumulative cost is at most (2+2/e)[I(f)-E I(leaf)]. Terminal K and the endpoint constant-mode entropy remain explicit.

Let R_nat(f)=sum_i M_i^+(f). Then 0<=B_nat(f)-R_nat(f)<=(1+2/e)I(f). More decisively, for every arbitrary Boolean core g and m>=1, F_m=y product_j g(x^(j)) satisfies R_nat(F_m)=m B_nat(g) and I(F_m)=mI(g)+1. Therefore a universal R_nat<=A I implies universal B_nat<=A I with the same constant. The optimal uniform constants for the two erasure quantities are equal. C018 consequently gives FEI in bits with constant (A+2)/ln2. No easier universal class has been introduced by deleting the empty row.

**First unsupported line:** there is a finite universal A with R_nat(f)<=A I(f) for every Boolean f, or a specified physical stopping tree with a summably paid M_i^+ residual and separately paid terminal entropy. This run pays the channel and singleton level terms, not that retained erasure residual.

For odd majority n=2m+1, alpha=binom(2m,m)/2^(2m), singleton channel and erasure are both zero, while K(parent)-E K(child)=alpha^2 ln(1/alpha^2)/2. Its ratio to queried influence is alpha ln(1/alpha)<=1/e. Thus a channel-only heat payment is false already at Maj_3. This does not pay the entropy preserved in its children.

For padded, point-defected Addr_a, equations (9)–(11) give exact three-class spectra and all padding-block charges for every a>=1, r>=1 and 1<=b<=r. For fixed a,b with r increasing, M_B/I_B and C_B/I_B both tend to 2ln2, while the heat-drop ratio tends to 2ln2. For the full padding block b=r, with k=a+2^a and rho=4^a/2^k, the respective limits are 2(1+rho)ln2 and 2(1-rho)ln2; the heat ratio remains 2ln2. The level bill has a nonzero full-block limit 4ln2/2^k per influence. These limits are proved analytically, not inferred from samples. Refining the block into singleton queries pays its level charges but retains the M_i^+ tree debt.

# A066 B14: scope repair and a sharper singleton heat budget

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every Boolean sign on every finite uniform cube, pay the physical singleton heat drop while retaining conditional erasure and terminal entropy. Natural logarithms throughout. The integral is K(f)=(H_nat(f)-p_empty ln(1/p_empty))/2, with J_f(t)=sum_S |S|p_S exp(-2t|S|)ln(1/p_S). Prior endpoint proof applies: for each finite f the integrand is bounded at zero and bounded by exp(-2t)J_f(0) at infinity. No dimension-independent integrable pointwise envelope is used. This stage repairs a scope error and proves a quantitative paid fragment; it does not prove FEI.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2; BOARD snapshot 2026-10-13 21:55 UTC; B14 STREAM_NOTES v2 (2026-10-11 23:25 UTC); own STATUS/LEARNED and prior heat/row derivations. A088 run 20261012T024518Z REPORT, claims and out/A066_BLOCK_SCOPE_CERTIFICATE.json: the unqualified v1 block-tree price is false, although the singleton proofs and all-block identity hold. A067 run 20261012T004014Z REPORT: its padding-access reserve is already handled and is not duplicated here. Source paths and byte hashes are in out/SOURCES.json. The stale lead target was completed in stage 2; protocol dispatch now consumes the useful A088 objection.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md supplies the proof. For every singleton i, set d=Inf_i, p=hat f({i})^2. Deleting the empty residual row gives C_i^+<=2(d-p). Combining its debit with ell_i=p ln(1/p), using sqrt(p)<=d and maximizing 2x(ln(1/x)-1), gives C_i^++ell_i<=(2+2/e^2)d. This replaces the prior separate charge 2+2/e by beta=2+2/e^2, gaining 2/e-2/e^2>0.46 nats per queried influence. The improved price telescopes ONLY over singleton queries.

The all-block identity remains exact. Refining B into singleton queries gives C_B^++ell_B=P_ref+D_ref, where D_ref=R_ref-M_B^+ is signed and P_ref<=beta I_B. This explicitly retains the missing conditional-erasure charge. In the all-n Boolean point-defect family, original block cost/I tends to 4ln2, refined paid cost/I to 2ln2, and retained transfer/I to 2ln2. The n128 objection is reconstructed with ln2>842/1215 and e>8/3; adding one irrelevant coordinate shows that merely requiring a proper block is insufficient.

The first unsupported line is a universal influence payment for the retained singleton erasure bill (and actual terminal entropy), or equivalently universal R_nat<=A I. Prior exact character/tensor admission shows that this is the same optimal-constant obstruction as C018 erasure B<=K I. This stage neither drops the signed transfer nor asserts its universal price.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Control restrictions preserve the actual family. Every finite adaptive control-only singleton stopping prefix therefore spends at most (256/9)delta times its removed physical influence; all terminal q_leaf/core entropy and K remain. This is a concrete history/reference correction, not a FEI counterfamily or a free flag code.

# A067 — local Boolean granularity instead of dimension-level counting

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Packet A067, stream B14, run 20261011T220539Z-account05-A067-hypercontractive-refinement. The target is a Boolean-specific improvement of a logarithmic entropy-conversion loss. For every finite uniform-cube Boolean sign f, let q_S=hat f(S)^2 and define d_f(S)=max_{T containing S, q_T>0}|T| on nonzero nonempty coefficients. Put U(f)=sum_nonempty q_S(d_f(S)-|S|). We prove

    H(f)<=2I(f)+2U(f)-(2-log2(e))Var(f).

Thus the measurable regime U<=KI, for a fixed finite K, satisfies FEI with constant 2+2K independently of dimension. The support condition d_f(S)<=L|S| is a sufficient, entropy-free structural test giving constant 2L. A balanced support antichain has H<=2I-2. The global degree-granularity special case and the antichain corollary are not asserted to be new in the literature. Entropy is in bits throughout this new theorem. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read the complete execution protocol, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, and the board snapshot written 2026-10-11 22:05 UTC. B14 STREAM_NOTES.md was absent at the supplied path; no direction was inferred from its absence. Read campaign README/CURRENT_STATUS and claim/obstruction navigation, the A067 assignment and B14 next-action contract, the campaign plan's targets and source guards, and the supplement's CAMPAIGN/CLAIMS/COUNTEREXAMPLES/SEED_LEMMAS/NATIVE_MAPPING. Large combined tool outputs were truncated; source reads below identify the mathematical portions actually used rather than asserting a full end-to-end audit of all background.

(29), around text lines 831-852. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md is the complete proof and scope analysis; BOOLEAN_HYPERCONTRACTIVE_REFINEMENT.tex is the packet's requested mathematical deliverable, and REGIME_COMPARISON.md gives the old/new comparison.

The exact general level-count step H(P_k)<=log2 binom(n,k)<=k log2(en/k) introduces a dimension logarithm. Hypercontractivity controls noise-weighted level mass but does not supply an upper bound on within-level entropy without additional structure. Our replacement uses Boolean vertex quantization: 2^(|S|-1)D_S f is integer-valued and has degree d_f(S)-|S|, giving hat f(S) in 2^(1-d_f(S)) Z. Its coefficient surprisal is therefore at most 2(d_f(S)-1). Summing and paying the empty atom proves the displayed estimate.

The point defect has U<=I and gives H<=4I-(2-log2(e))Var, while the generic marginal estimate loses a ratio of order n. Level counting is tight on that family; an initial contrary comparison was corrected before finalization. The strict level-count improvement uses the existing addressing baseline: U=a/2 and I=1+a/2 give the exact bound H<=2a, versus a generic full-cube level-count bound Theta(a^2). This does not reopen or claim the addressing theorem as new. The actual point-defect H/I->4 and fixed-order Renyi failures are recovered calibration facts. The real singleton spectrum, and even an L2-small real perturbation of a constant sign, fail the new bound when Boolean quantization is removed.

Two actual-Boolean obstructions identify the limit of this mechanism. A one-point flip of a dictator has I->1 but U~n. A single optimized character anchor repairs that example, but it still fails on Maj_3 tensor a point defect: every character-anchored arithmetic envelope is >=3n/2, whereas I<=5/2 and H=2+o(1). These refute helper payments, not FEI. The proof handles every anchor, including anchors with zero coefficient, and retains the whole tensor factor.

The first unsupported universal line is the existence of a tail-stable arithmetic charge or fully paid partition with cost O(I) for every Boolean source. A universal bound on U or on the optimized single-anchor envelope is now refuted, rather than left as a hopeful gap. Physical restrictions are Boolean, but the U regime has not been proved hereditary.

Section 2 uses Boolean coefficient granularity and a scalar second difference to prove the fixed-seed padding-node ratio tends to 4, with explicit bound 4Lp(|m+r-3|+m+3/(2ln2)). Section 3 proves every point-defect node has rate <=5, handling dimensions 1,2,3 exactly. Section 4 constructs U<=I stopping trees for dictator surgery and Maj3 tensor point defects; their complete ledger has rate 5. These known FEI classes are calibration of the access mechanism, not the main advance.

Section 5 proves a scope obstruction: for an even-dimensional quadratic bent seed at fixed padding r, the node rate grows with slope 2^(1-r) in seed dimension. The explicit m=2,r=2 rate already exceeds 5. This is not an unbounded H/I family.

Section 6 sums padding corrections before paying them. Expected queried influence is the same d at each surviving padding depth. The aggregate correction is at most (1+6/ln2)md; cube edge-connectivity and the exact one-vertex pivot change prove md<=I_core, including constant seeds. The terminal certificate and this single reserve combine into one coefficient on expected leaf influence. No reserve is renewed at descendants. Section 7 records finite edited-tribes tests. Their last-padding nodes can exceed rate 5, so even padding strips cannot inherit the point-defect rate for arbitrary seeds.

The first unsupported line for universal FEI is a dimension-independent payment of access inside an arbitrary active core to U-certified leaves, with the entire remaining entropy retained. An unmodified arbitrary seed is a positive-probability clean leaf of the padding tree; treating that leaf as paid would simply assume the missing theorem.

# A067 stage 3: final-mask ownership for shared-core edits

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Pay C019 access for two rare graph-controlled edits sharing an arbitrary Boolean core and control block. Let g(x) be Boolean, z contain r dummy controls, and F=g*(-1)^1_E use the actual final edit mask. Sequential flips use XOR; union-once edits use union. If every core fiber has at most k assignments and k*2^-r<=1/4, the proved universal access statement is

    H(F)-E_z H(F_z) <= K I_controls(F)+2 I_core(F),
    K=4r/(r-log2 k)+2/ln2.

For one assignment per fiber and r>=2, K=4+2/ln2<7. For two assignments per fiber and r>=3, K<=6+2/ln2<9. Every quantity belongs to the final F. One reserve is charged across the whole control tree. The theorem allows arbitrary seed dimension, edit domains and graph control maps, and both events may share their complete active core. It does not admit arbitrary terminal core leaves.

If every actual full-reveal leaf satisfies U<=bI, the complete entropy ledger yields H(F)<=max(K,4+2b)I(F). Thus b=1 gives the same <7/<9 constants in the two regimes. Early U stopping retains remaining control influence and has the larger coefficient K+2+2b. All information is in bits, all physical functions are Boolean signs on uniform cubes. Full FEI remains OPEN.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md gives the entire proof. Entropy tensorization, the two-point scalar channel, Boolean edge isoperimetry and the full physical restriction identity are reconstructed on the page. The channel bound J_controls<=2 I_controls/ln2 is already dimension-free and extends to real coefficient fields; it is not the novel Boolean payment.

The nonempty-control spectral mass is exactly alpha=E_x[4a_x(1-a_x)], with a_x the final fiber density. Its empty/nonempty flag gives the control erasure price at most 8r*rho+4rho*log2(1/epsilon), where rho is total edit probability and epsilon is active-core support density. Control-fiber isoperimetry pays the first part at rate 4r/(r-log2 k). The new Boolean ownership step pays the second: on each core edge the edit mismatch probability is <=1/2, so the final F edge is pivotal with at least that probability irrespective of the seed edge's sign. Thus I_core(F)>=I_core(final mask)>=2rho*log2(1/epsilon). This charges a reserve 2I_core ONCE after cancellation, never the sum of two separate ancestral reserves.

All actual leaf entropy remains in H(F)=Q+E H(F_z). The U certificate is conditional, and the early-tree proof keeps unrevealed control influence rather than replacing root control influence by spent influence. No small-fiber hypothesis is silently imposed again at descendants. A one-core/one-control-bit Boolean witness refutes the final-edge ownership comparison when the sparse-fiber cap is removed.

The precise dimension loss is the conditional label step H(S_controls|flag,core)<=r, compared with control-fiber physical influence 2rho(r-log2 k). It yields the displayed r/(r-log2 k), not a logarithm introduced by the real log-Sobolev channel. On a codimension-two subcube with r-2 irrelevant controls, H=2 and I=1 exactly, but the crude flag alphabet bound grows as h2(3/4)+(3/4)r. This isolates a removable dimension overcount. Unequal overlapping large fibers need a conditional source-compatible reference after irrelevant controls are contracted.

The first unsupported universal line is a uniformly paid large-fiber conditional reference and/or actual arbitrary-core leaf admission, with the same final-core reserve owned once. The main access theorem is complete at its stated scope; no line in its proof assumes FEI.

One final control reference gives B_Z<=h2(alpha)+alpha log2 M+2I_Z. Alpha<=I_Z and the flag lemma pay it; add the real channel price. The complete joint reference uses the actual final core marginal, whose entropy equals the retained terminal entropy plus that channel. No terminal or ancestral bank is erased.

This removes both former hypotheses inside the two-controller algebra, including fully exposed canceled events. It does not permit arbitrary source dependence outside that algebra. First unsupported universal line: pay the retained 2I_X reserve from genuinely unused child slack or reduce it through a sharper conditional reference; H(child)<=C I(child) alone yields C+2, so it cannot close universal same-C induction. A096's paid quotient loss is not an unused terminal surplus.

SOURCE agents\A068\runs\20261011T222313Z-account05-A068-antichain-beyond-one-order\REPORT.md
# A068 / B14: context-dependent antichains with quadratic spectral fibers

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let h:{-1,1}^q -> {-1,1} be regular in the published sense: after fixed coordinate orientations it is monotone and has a total desirability order. Let z consist of m independent uniform bits, write z_j=(-1)^{u_j}, and let Q_1,...,Q_q be arbitrary polynomials of degree at most two over F_2 in u. They may share every control variable. Put a_i(z)=(-1)^{Q_i(u)} and

    F(x,z)=h(x_1 a_1(z),...,x_q a_q(z)).

The x_i are distinct private physical data coordinates, independent of z. For every finite q,m, with no bound on the number, degree of overlap, or rank of the quadratic controls,

    H(F) <= C_reg I_x(F)+2 I_z(F) <= C_reg I(F),
    C_reg=log_2(4+e)+2log_2(e*pi)<8.937.

Here I_x=sum_i Inf_{x_i}, I_z=sum_j Inf_{z_j}; entropy is in bits. The sharper consequence H(F)<=C_reg D_x(F)+2I_z(F), where D_x=sum_i min(Inf_{x_i},1-Inf_{x_i}), follows from the recovered centered regular theorem. The ordinary inequality above is reconstructed with a complete normalized reference below. This is a proper-class result, not full FEI. Its local proof and exact validation are complete; independent acceptance is pending. Novelty outside the read corpus is unverified.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full (fei-execution-protocol/2, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2), README.md, CURRENT_STATUS.md, CLAIM_REGISTRY.json, OBSTRUCTION_REGISTRY.json, PACKET_STATUS.json, STREAM_NEXT_ACTIONS.json, and the B14/A068 portions of FEI_100_agent_research_plan.tex. Read the three earlier run REPORT.md files under B05, B09, and B19. BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 22:16 UTC was read at stage start. B14 STREAM_NOTES.md did not exist at its prescribed location; no lead direction was inferred.

Read supplemental CAMPAIGN.md, CLAIMS.md, COUNTEREXAMPLES.md, SEED_LEMMAS.md and NATIVE_MAPPING.md, plus source excerpts ERASURE_REDUCTION.md, OVERLAPPING_PRODUCT_NOTE.md and ROUTE_MAP_CURATED.md. The last source's Section 2.1 already supplies signed physical-translation symmetry removal at zero entropy decrement. Accordingly the linear-control special case is a recovered-mechanism application, not a new mechanism claimed here. The nonlinear quadratic extension has a positive conditional entropy term which that reduction does not eliminate.

Mathematical dependencies are the current threshold source, SHA256 5d87f625fbadad4aa9841416712b8193a869fa9dffcb4e9c200d2f39a62e6a10, baseline/current-threshold-paper.tex: lem:pivotal-antichain, lem:antichain-density, lem:antichain-noise, lem:ranktail (mean 1+ln 2), lem:levelentropy, eq:euler-reference, eq:euler-cost, thm:A, and the ordinary combination coefficient eq:multiI. These passages were read directly. Exact hashes and read scope are saved in out/SOURCES.json. Non-UTF8 paper text was decoded with replacement/CP1252 as needed. No web or novelty search was performed.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. The replacement pivotal geometry

Fix z and write y_i=x_i a_i(z), also incorporating any fixed orientations of h. The map x -> y is a coordinate orientation, preserving uniform measure. For data pivot x_i,

    d_{x_i} F(x_{-i},z)=a_i(z) d_i h(y_{-i}).

In the coordinates preceding i in h's desirability order, every fixed-z pivotal fiber is therefore an antichain in the orientation a_j(z). The orientation is allowed to depend on the exterior control assignment, but not on the resampled predecessor coordinates. For every resampling mask R, its restricted fiber is still an antichain. Thus the signed relative-noise estimate remains valid after averaging z. This is the precise sufficient condition replacing one globally fixed orientation. It does not imply that F is globally unate.

For rank i, U_i={1,...,i-1} after reordering, the normalized derivative spectrum of F satisfies

    E (1-t)^{|T intersect U_i|} <= E c_B,
    c_b=2^{-b} binom(b,floor(b/2)), B~Bin(i-1,t).

Indeed bound d(X)d(Y) by |d(X)||d(Y)| and, conditionally on X,z,R, bound the latter support probability by c_|R|. Since R is independent of X,z, the derivative mass factors out. Signs of derivatives are harmless. The Fourier partial-noise identity diagonalizes resampling exactly as in the baseline. The same harmonic integral then gives

    E ln[i/(|T intersect U_i|+1)] <= sum_{b=1}^{i-1} c_b/b <= 1+ln 2.

For completeness, E h_kappa = integral_0^1 [1-E(1-t)^kappa] dt/t is at least sum_{b=1}^{i-1}(1-c_b)/b. Also ln[i/(kappa+1)] <= h_{i-1}-h_kappa. Finally c_{2j-1}=c_{2j}=4^{-j}binom(2j,j), and the binomial series yields sum_{j>=1} c_{2j}/(2j-1)=1 and sum_{j>=1} c_{2j}/(2j)=ln 2. This is the recovered sharp rank mean, now applied to conditional orientations.

### 2. Exact data-indexed packets; no incoherent replacement

For S subset [q], let a_S=product_{i in S} a_i, Q_S=sum_{i in S} Q_i over F_2. Expanding h gives the exact identity

    F(x,z)=sum_S hhat(S) chi_S(x) a_S(z),
    Fhat(S,T)=hhat(S) ahat_S(T).

Distinct S have disjoint full Fourier indices because their physical data labels differ. Hence there is no coherent sum to discard. Define p_S=hhat(S)^2 and nu_S(T)=ahat_S(T)^2. Parseval gives sum_T nu_S(T)=1 even when hhat(S)=0. Then

    P_F(S,T)=p_S nu_S(T),
    H(F)=H(h)+sum_S p_S H(a_S),
    I_x(F)=I(h),
    I_z(F)=sum_S p_S I(a_S).

These identities require distinct private data bits. They are valid for arbitrary Boolean controls; the next step uses degree at most two.

### 3. Quadratic control packets are affine flats with a paid rank

Let a(u)=(-1)^{Q(u)} for a polynomial Q of degree at most two on F_2^m. Let B be its alternating polar matrix, so

    Q(u+v)+Q(u)+Q(v)+Q(0)=u^T B v,
    r=rank_F2(B), R=ker(B).

The spectral characteristic function is its physical autocorrelation:

    sum_T ahat(T)^2 (-1)^{T dot v}
       = E_u a(u)a(u+v)
       = 1_{v in R} (-1)^{Q(v)+Q(0)}.

On R, Q(v)+Q(0) is linear because its polarization vanishes. Choose t_0 extending this functional. Walsh inversion gives

    nu(T)=2^{-r} 1_{T in t_0+R_perp}.

Thus nu is uniform on an affine space of dimension r, and H(a)=r. Let J be the coordinate support of the linear space R_perp. Every coordinate in J is uniform on the affine space; the remaining coordinates are fixed. Since dim(R_perp)=r, |J|>=r. Therefore

    I(a)=E_nu |T| >= |J|/2 >= r/2,
    H(a)=r <= 2 I(a).

This includes r=0, affine-linear controls and constant controls: their spectra are single atoms and their entropy is zero. We do not treat a non-Boolean normalized vector as a quadratic phase. The displayed Boolean autocorrelation proves the affine-flat assertion.

For each data set S, write L_S=t_S+ker(B_S)_perp and r_S=rank B_S. The product a_S remains a quadratic phase, so its full entropy bill r_S is paid by twice its physical control influence. The geometry is a family of context-oriented pivotal cubes over the control cube, with affine spectral fibers of possibly different dimensions over data sets.

### 4. Complete normalized rank reference and all its costs

Ranks are relabeled i=1,...,q. On data sets of size k define

    E_k(S)=product_{i in S} i^{-2},
    Z_k=sum_{|S|=k} E_k(S),
    R_k(S)=E_k(S)/Z_k.

Z_k is finite and strictly positive for 0<=k<=q, including Z_0=1. Euler's product from the baseline bounds Z_k<=pi^{2k}/(2k+1)!. Thus, if S={i_1<...<i_k},

    -log_2 R_k(S)
      <= (2/ln 2) sum_{j=1}^k ln(i_j/j)+k log_2(pi^2/4).

The factorial comparison (2k+1)!>=4^k(k!)^2 follows by induction. Define the full conditional reference

    R_k^F(S,T)=R_k(S) 2^{-r_S} 1_{T in L_S}, |S|=k.

For each S, L_S has exactly 2^{r_S} points. Therefore sum_{S,T} R_k^F(S,T)=1. It covers all positive Fourier atoms. Let w_k=P_h(|S|=k) and R^F(S,T)=w_|S| R_|S|^F(S,T). This too is a normalized probability law. Zero w_k levels carry zero actual mass and contribute zero.

The full reference cost is

    log_2(1/w_k) -log_2 R_k(S) + r_S.

In particular, the control geometry is not discarded: its conditional affine rank is charged once per complete Fourier set. The fixed polynomial list and coordinate order are part of the reference instance known to the decoder. Given S, the decoder computes B_S and L_S and decodes an r_S-bit affine coordinate. No extra choice of geometry is made separately for each observed set. If an unknown representation is selected from several lists, a normalized prior and its selection cost would additionally be required; this theorem does not waive that cost.

Gibbs' inequality, the derivative law and Step 1 now give

    H(F) <= H(|S|)+(2/ln 2)(1+ln 2) I_x
                    +log_2(pi^2/4) I_x + E_p r_S.

The spectral-level entropy of h is at most log_2(4+e) I(h)=log_2(4+e) I_x by the recovered Boolean level lemma. Step 3 gives E_p r_S<=2I_z. Consequently

    H(F) <= [log_2(4+e)+2log_2(e*pi)] I_x+2I_z.

This proves the claimed complete rank-reference theorem. Alternatively Step 2 plus H(h)<=C_reg D(h) gives the stated D_x refinement directly. No unsupported line occurs in the class theorem. For arbitrary controls, the first unsupported universal line would instead be sum_S p_S H(a_S)<=K sum_S p_S I(a_S) with absolute K. Taking h to be a dictator reduces that proposed line to full FEI on an arbitrary control a_1; the quadratic hypothesis is essential to the current proof.

# A068 / B14 stage 2: a full-support rank reference surviving one-point edits

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every odd q>=5 let z_j=(-1)^{u_j} and

    F_q(x,z)=MAJ_q(x_i (-1)^{u_i u_{i+1}} : i=1,...,q),

with cyclic indices and 2q independent uniform physical sign bits. Pad with d>=0 unused bits, giving N=2q+d, and flip F_q at any one input v. Denote the edited Boolean function by G and delta=2^{-N}. The x block has q data coordinates; the z block includes the q original controls and all padding coordinates. All entropy and reference costs are in bits.

This stage proves a complete normalized full-support reference R_delta with

    H(G) <= Gamma_delta(G) := sum_A Ghat(A)^2 log_2(1/R_delta(A))
          <= C_reg I_x(G)+2 I_z(G) - (287/1920) I_x(F_q),
    C_reg=log_2(4+e)+2log_2(e*pi)<8.937.

In particular the original numerical block bound survives every single-point edit and every amount of padding for this cyclic family. The exact quadratic representation need not survive the edit. The original support-only reference does not remain valid unchanged; the full-support repair and its entire expected cost are essential. This is an edit-stable proper-class C020 payment, not universal FEI. The all-parameter proof is below; independent review of the new extension is pending.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json in full, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2. Read BOARD.md supervisor snapshot 2026-10-12 02:32 UTC, B14 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 dated 2026-10-11 23:25 UTC, and own STATUS.md/LEARNED.md and stage-1 REPORT.md. The lead specifically assigns A068 exact q=5,7 cyclic replay and a point-defected member, so that robustness obligation takes precedence over the prior proposed private-data identification.

Read A088's stage-1 REPORT.md and A068 section in AUDIT.md. It independently accepts A068-QUADRATIC-CONTEXT-RANK-REFERENCE v1 at its precise private-data/quadratic-sign/recovered-C001 scope, including the normalized reference. Its independent exact validation covered the q=5 cyclic example. No A094 acceptance is inferred. A070's scope audit also inspected A068's structural hypotheses but explicitly did not claim a full A068 audit.

Read A069's stable-hierarchy edit-payment stage-1 REPORT.md, Sections 1-4. Its weighted L2 transfer for fixed nonnegative complete code costs and physical edit-influence bound are established tools and already independently reconstructed by A070. The present stage uses the same weighted triangle mechanism, and credits that source; it does not claim to invent perturbation transfer or repeat recursive-majority neighborhoods. The new work is a normalized uniform fallback for a previously support-only quadratic rank reference, and proof that the balanced cyclic margin pays it while preserving the same block coefficient.

The recovered baseline ingredients are the threshold source lem:ranktail/eq:euler-cost and its reference normalization, plus C_reg. The source used in stage 1 is baseline/current-threshold-paper.tex, SHA256 5d87f625fbadad4aa9841416712b8193a869fa9dffcb4e9c200d2f39a62e6a10. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independently reconstructed cyclic influences

Put c_q=2^{-(q-1)} binom(q-1,(q-1)/2). Conditional on controls the transformed data signs are independent uniform, so every data influence is c_q. Flipping u_j toggles vote j-1 when u_{j-1}=1 and vote j when u_{j+1}=1. Thus zero, one and two votes toggle with probabilities 1/4,1/2,1/4. Toggling one vote changes majority with probability c_q. Toggling two changes it precisely when those votes agree and the remaining q-2 votes sum to +/-1, also giving c_q. Consequently

    I_x(F_q)=q c_q=:J,
    I_z(F_q)=(3/4)J.

At q=5 these are 15/8 and 45/32; at q=7 they are 35/16 and 105/64. Padding does not change them. The central-binomial recurrence is c_{q+2}/c_q=q/(q+1), so

    (q+2)c_{q+2}/(q c_q)=(q+2)/(q+1)>1,
    J>=15/8 for every odd q>=5.

These formulas are analytic for all q, with independent exact replay at the assigned dimensions in the checker.

### 2. The balanced base reference has a strict saved level cost

Let R_0 be the stage-1 normalized rank/affine-fiber reference for F_q, embedded on the N-dimensional spectrum by putting no Fourier coordinates in the padding block. In data rank order, for |S|=k and control index T,

    R_0(S,T)=w_k [prod_{i in S} i^{-2}/Z_k] 2^{-r_S} 1_{T in L_S},
    Z_k=sum_{|S|=k} prod_{i in S} i^{-2},

where L_S is the affine Fourier support of the quadratic control phase indexed by S, r_S=dim L_S, and w_k is the base majority spectral level mass. It is a probability law: the rank factor normalizes on each level, each affine fiber has 2^{r_S} points, and sum_k w_k=1. Its expected cost charges level selection, rank normalizer, all data ranks and all control affine coordinates exactly as in the accepted stage-1 proof.

Majority is balanced, so w_0=0 and the data spectral level K is positive. The normalized geometric reference 2^{-k}, k>=1, gives H(K)<=E K=J by Gibbs. Replacing the general Boolean level cost C_lev J by this stronger balanced cost in the same Euler rank proof gives

    Gamma_0(F_q) <= B := C_bal J+2 I_z(F_q),
    C_bal=1+2log_2(e*pi)=C_reg-C_lev+1,
    C_lev=log_2(4+e).

Here C_lev>5/2 because 4+e>6>sqrt(32), while C_reg<9. Since I_z=3J/4,

    B=(C_bal+3/2)J<9J,
    C_reg J+2I_z-B=(C_lev-1)J>(3/2)J.

This balanced margin is the budget for the edit. It is not earned again downstream and does not assume the edited function is balanced.

### 3. Uniform fallback; all normalization and edit cross terms retained

Let U_N(A)=2^{-N} on every spectral subset. Define

    R_delta(A)=(1-delta)R_0(A)+delta U_N(A), delta=2^{-N}.

Both branches are normalized, so sum_A R_delta(A)=1; R_delta(A)>=delta 2^{-N}=2^{-2N} at every atom. Its cost L_delta=-log_2 R_delta is nonnegative and at most 2N. For the unedited F_q,

    Gamma_delta(F_q) <= Gamma_0(F_q)+log_2(1/(1-delta))
                       <= B+3delta.

The last estimate follows from -ln(1-delta)<=delta/(1-delta), log_2 e<3/2 and delta<=1/1024. The small base-branch selection cost has not been dropped.

Write e=G-F_q. Its exact one-point Fourier coefficients are

    ehat(A)=-2 F_q(v) delta chi_A(v),
    ehat(A)^2=4delta^2.

Thus its entire weighted edit-spectrum cost is

    sum_A ehat(A)^2 L_delta(A)
       =4delta E_{U_N} L_delta <=8Ndelta.

Weighted Euclidean Minkowski, applied before squaring, gives

    sqrt(Gamma_delta(G))
       <=sqrt(Gamma_delta(F_q))+sqrt(sum_A ehat(A)^2 L_delta(A)),
    Gamma_delta(G) <= (sqrt(B+3delta)+sqrt(8Ndelta))^2.

This keeps the signed coherent cross terms. It is not the false entropy subadditivity of an incoherent mixture. Since R_delta is normalized and positive everywhere, Gibbs gives H(G)<=Gamma_delta(G), even though an edit fills atoms excluded by the original geometry. The reference really describes the complete spectral set, including new padding and empty-data atoms.

### 4. Exact rational payment by the saved margin

For N>=2q>=10, N 2^{-N} is decreasing. Using J>=15/8,

    a:=3delta/J <=1/640,
    b:=8Ndelta/J <=1/24.

B/J<9 implies

    (Gamma_delta(G)-B)/J
       <=a+b+2sqrt[b(9+a)] <=1/640+1/24+5/4.

The final inequality is an exact square comparison:

    4(1/24)(9+1/640)=5761/3840 <25/16=(5/4)^2.

A one-point edit changes each physical coordinate influence by at most 2delta; more precisely its change is 2delta(1-2b_i), where b_i indicates a base disagreeing neighbor at the edited input. Each padding influence becomes 2delta. Ignoring these positive padding contributions for the lower bound,

    C_reg I_x(G)+2I_z(G)
       >=C_reg J+2I_z(F_q)-2qdelta(C_reg+2).

Since C_reg+2<11 and 2q<=N,

    [2qdelta(C_reg+2)]/J <=11Ndelta/J <=11/192.

Combining all costs with the balanced saving gives

    [C_reg I_x(G)+2I_z(G)-Gamma_delta(G)]/J
       >3/2 -1/640 -1/24 -5/4 -11/192
       =287/1920.

This proves the exact all-q/all-padding/all-location claim. Every rare-reference, rank, level and edit charge is in one normalized reference. No terminal core entropy is discarded.

For fixed q and N tending to infinity by padding, the reference-selection cost and weighted edit cost tend to zero. On each original positive atom R_delta converges to R_0, and the finitely many original positive atoms give Gamma_delta(F_q)->Gamma_0(F_q). The weighted triangle bound then gives Gamma_delta(G)->Gamma_0(F_q). Thus the paid functional passes the pad-and-flip test rather than diverging when zeros are filled.

### 5. The exact structural obstruction and remaining unsupported line

At the all-positive input with u=0, F_q=+1. Flipping that value makes the x_i derivative at the remaining all-positive data assignment equal to -1. It is nonzero also at the ordinary central majority pivotal assignments, equal to +1 there. For the final data rank these assignments are comparable to the all-positive assignment in the predecessor cube, so its pivotal support is no longer an antichain in the original conditional orientation. One cannot simply reapply the original fiber proof to the edited function. The old R_0 also has zero mass on all empty-data atoms because w_0=0; the edited function has a nonzero empty Fourier coefficient. Hence R_0 is not even an admissible reference after that edit.

The new statement is an explicit repair, not an assertion that arbitrary point edits preserve quadratic-gate membership. No theorem in stage 1 is refuted or silently strengthened to all edits of every regular/quadratic core.

First unsupported universal line: after private data labels are identified, the signed colliding quadratic packets admit a complete reference with a sufficient paid margin and no dimension-sized fallback bill. The present proof uses both private-label packet structure and the balanced cyclic saving. It gives no universal rank-reference existence theorem, no arbitrary-control FEI claim, and no same-constant edit theorem for every FEI-certified class without a strict saved budget.

Padding and all added spectral atoms are accounted for exactly by the coefficient formula and direct local edge updates.

Positive ln 2 division uses the correct outward endpoints. Every edit is certified against the stronger rational coefficient 8I_x+2I_z; C_reg>8 follows from (4+e)e^2 pi^2>6*7*9>256. Output margins are rounded downward to 10^{-12}; entropy/cost endpoints are rounded outward. No floating-point entropy, scan fit or dimension extrapolation is used as proof.

# A068 / B14 stage 3: a full-support rank reference surviving one-point edits

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

For every odd q>=5 let z_j=(-1)^{u_j} and

    F_q(x,z)=MAJ_q(x_i (-1)^{u_i u_{i+1}} : i=1,...,q),

with cyclic indices and 2q independent uniform physical sign bits. Pad with d>=0 unused bits, giving N=2q+d, and flip F_q at any one input v. Denote the edited Boolean function by G and delta=2^{-N}. The x block has q data coordinates; the z block includes the q original controls and all padding coordinates. All entropy and reference costs are in bits.

This stage proves a complete normalized full-support reference R_delta with

    H(G) <= Gamma_delta(G) := sum_A Ghat(A)^2 log_2(1/R_delta(A))
          <= C_reg I_x(G)+2 I_z(G) - (287/1920) I_x(F_q),
    C_reg=log_2(4+e)+2log_2(e*pi)<8.937.

In particular the original numerical block bound survives every single-point edit and every amount of padding for this cyclic family. The exact quadratic representation need not survive the edit. The original support-only reference does not remain valid unchanged; the full-support repair and its entire expected cost are essential. This is an edit-stable proper-class C020 payment, not universal FEI. The all-parameter proof is below. A070 independently accepted this unchanged theorem from the predecessor report; the full author validation package is completed here.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Independently reconstructed cyclic influences

Put c_q=2^{-(q-1)} binom(q-1,(q-1)/2). Conditional on controls the transformed data signs are independent uniform, so every data influence is c_q. Flipping u_j toggles vote j-1 when u_{j-1}=1 and vote j when u_{j+1}=1. Thus zero, one and two votes toggle with probabilities 1/4,1/2,1/4. Toggling one vote changes majority with probability c_q. Toggling two changes it precisely when those votes agree and the remaining q-2 votes sum to +/-1, also giving c_q. Consequently

    I_x(F_q)=q c_q=:J,
    I_z(F_q)=(3/4)J.

At q=5 these are 15/8 and 45/32; at q=7 they are 35/16 and 105/64. Padding does not change them. The central-binomial recurrence is c_{q+2}/c_q=q/(q+1), so

    (q+2)c_{q+2}/(q c_q)=(q+2)/(q+1)>1,
    J>=15/8 for every odd q>=5.

These formulas are analytic for all q, with independent exact replay at the assigned dimensions in the checker.

### 2. The balanced base reference has a strict saved level cost

Let R_0 be the stage-1 normalized rank/affine-fiber reference for F_q, embedded on the N-dimensional spectrum by putting no Fourier coordinates in the padding block. In data rank order, for |S|=k and control index T,

    R_0(S,T)=w_k [prod_{i in S} i^{-2}/Z_k] 2^{-r_S} 1_{T in L_S},
    Z_k=sum_{|S|=k} prod_{i in S} i^{-2},

where L_S is the affine Fourier support of the quadratic control phase indexed by S, r_S=dim L_S, and w_k is the base majority spectral level mass. It is a probability law: the rank factor normalizes on each level, each affine fiber has 2^{r_S} points, and sum_k w_k=1. Its expected cost charges level selection, rank normalizer, all data ranks and all control affine coordinates exactly as in the accepted stage-1 proof.

Majority is balanced, so w_0=0 and the data spectral level K is positive. The normalized geometric reference 2^{-k}, k>=1, gives H(K)<=E K=J by Gibbs. Replacing the general Boolean level cost C_lev J by this stronger balanced cost in the same Euler rank proof gives

    Gamma_0(F_q) <= B := C_bal J+2 I_z(F_q),
    C_bal=1+2log_2(e*pi)=C_reg-C_lev+1,
    C_lev=log_2(4+e).

Here C_lev>5/2 because 4+e>6>sqrt(32), while C_reg<9. Since I_z=3J/4,

    B=(C_bal+3/2)J<9J,
    C_reg J+2I_z-B=(C_lev-1)J>(3/2)J.

This balanced margin is the budget for the edit. It is not earned again downstream and does not assume the edited function is balanced.

### 3. Uniform fallback; all normalization and edit cross terms retained

Let U_N(A)=2^{-N} on every spectral subset. Define

    R_delta(A)=(1-delta)R_0(A)+delta U_N(A), delta=2^{-N}.

Both branches are normalized, so sum_A R_delta(A)=1; R_delta(A)>=delta 2^{-N}=2^{-2N} at every atom. Its cost L_delta=-log_2 R_delta is nonnegative and at most 2N. For the unedited F_q,

    Gamma_delta(F_q) <= Gamma_0(F_q)+log_2(1/(1-delta))
                       <= B+3delta.

The last estimate follows from -ln(1-delta)<=delta/(1-delta), log_2 e<3/2 and delta<=1/1024. The small base-branch selection cost has not been dropped.

Write e=G-F_q. Its exact one-point Fourier coefficients are

    ehat(A)=-2 F_q(v) delta chi_A(v),
    ehat(A)^2=4delta^2.

Thus its entire weighted edit-spectrum cost is

    sum_A ehat(A)^2 L_delta(A)
       =4delta E_{U_N} L_delta <=8Ndelta.

Weighted Euclidean Minkowski, applied before squaring, gives

    sqrt(Gamma_delta(G))
       <=sqrt(Gamma_delta(F_q))+sqrt(sum_A ehat(A)^2 L_delta(A)),
    Gamma_delta(G) <= (sqrt(B+3delta)+sqrt(8Ndelta))^2.

This keeps the signed coherent cross terms. It is not the false entropy subadditivity of an incoherent mixture. Since R_delta is normalized and positive everywhere, Gibbs gives H(G)<=Gamma_delta(G), even though an edit fills atoms excluded by the original geometry. The reference really describes the complete spectral set, including new padding and empty-data atoms.

### 4. Exact rational payment by the saved margin

For N>=2q>=10, N 2^{-N} is decreasing. Using J>=15/8,

    a:=3delta/J <=1/640,
    b:=8Ndelta/J <=1/24.

B/J<9 implies

    (Gamma_delta(G)-B)/J
       <=a+b+2sqrt[b(9+a)] <=1/640+1/24+5/4.

The final inequality is an exact square comparison:

    4(1/24)(9+1/640)=5761/3840 <25/16=(5/4)^2.

A one-point edit changes each physical coordinate influence by at most 2delta; more precisely its change is 2delta(1-2b_i), where b_i indicates a base disagreeing neighbor at the edited input. Each padding influence becomes 2delta. Ignoring these positive padding contributions for the lower bound,

    C_reg I_x(G)+2I_z(G)
       >=C_reg J+2I_z(F_q)-2qdelta(C_reg+2).

Since C_reg+2<11 and 2q<=N,

    [2qdelta(C_reg+2)]/J <=11Ndelta/J <=11/192.

Combining all costs with the balanced saving gives

    [C_reg I_x(G)+2I_z(G)-Gamma_delta(G)]/J
       >3/2 -1/640 -1/24 -5/4 -11/192
       =287/1920.

This proves the exact all-q/all-padding/all-location claim. Every rare-reference, rank, level and edit charge is in one normalized reference. No terminal core entropy is discarded.

For fixed q and N tending to infinity by padding, the reference-selection cost and weighted edit cost tend to zero. On each original positive atom R_delta converges to R_0, and the finitely many original positive atoms give Gamma_delta(F_q)->Gamma_0(F_q). The forward and reverse weighted triangle inequalities give |sqrt(Gamma_delta(G))-sqrt(Gamma_delta(F_q))|<=sqrt(8Ndelta), hence Gamma_delta(G)->Gamma_0(F_q). Thus the paid functional passes the pad-and-flip test rather than diverging when zeros are filled.

### 5. The exact structural obstruction and remaining unsupported line

At the all-positive input with u=0, F_q=+1. Flipping that value makes the x_i derivative at the remaining all-positive data assignment equal to -1. It is nonzero also at the ordinary central majority pivotal assignments, equal to +1 there. For the final data rank these assignments are comparable to the all-positive assignment in the predecessor cube, so its pivotal support is no longer an antichain in the original conditional orientation. One cannot simply reapply the original fiber proof to the edited function. The old R_0 also has zero mass on all empty-data atoms because w_0=0; the edited function has a nonzero empty Fourier coefficient. Hence R_0 is not even an admissible reference after that edit.

The new statement is an explicit repair, not an assertion that arbitrary point edits preserve quadratic-gate membership. No theorem in stage 1 is refuted or silently strengthened to all edits of every regular/quadratic core.

First unsupported universal line: after private data labels are identified, the signed colliding quadratic packets admit a complete reference with a sufficient paid margin and no dimension-sized fallback bill. The present proof uses both private-label packet structure and the balanced cyclic saving. It gives no universal rank-reference existence theorem, no arbitrary-control FEI claim, and no same-constant edit theorem for every FEI-certified class without a strict saved budget.

### 6. One identified private label: signed interface and failed inherited payment

For any Boolean F with distinct data coordinates 1,2 and arbitrary other coordinates, substitute x_2=epsilon x_1, epsilon in {+1,-1}, obtaining G. Index quotient subsets T by representatives excluding coordinate 2. Direct character substitution gives

    Ghat(T)=Fhat(T)+epsilon Fhat(T symmetric_difference {1,2}).

For any normalized parent reference R covering F's spectrum, define

    R_push(T)=R(T)+R(T symmetric_difference {1,2}).

The two-atom fibers partition the parent spectrum, so R_push is normalized and covers G. It satisfies Gamma_push(G)<=2 Gamma_R(F): use (a+epsilon b)^2<=2(a^2+b^2) and -log R_push(T)<=-log R(A) for either parent atom A. Terms with zero Fourier mass are omitted. This pays the parent cost, not the physical influence of G.

The naive universal replacement Gamma_push(G)<=K I(G) fails for every finite K. Take regular h=AND2 (output +1 only at (+1,+1)), constant quadratic orientations (+1,-1), and F(x_1,x_2)=h(x_1,-x_2). In spectral order empty,{1},{2},{1,2}, its coefficients are (-1/2,+1/2,-1/2,-1/2). Its accepted rank reference has weights (1/4,2/5,1/10,1/4), from level masses (1/4,1/2,1/4) and singleton rank weights proportional to (1,1/4). Identifying x_2=x_1 gives G=-1 but R_push=(1/2,1/2). Thus H(G)=I(G)=0 and Gamma_push(G)=1 bit. G itself has a free reference concentrated at the empty atom. This refutes the inherited payment, not FEI or existence of an adaptive paid output reference.

The frozen exhaustive 32-case two-bit check verifies the signed coefficient identity, factor-two inequality, Boolean normalization and influence with fresh truth tables, plus this exact reference witness. It does not certify larger colliding quadratic packets. The first unsupported line is a cancellation-aware normalized replacement reference paid by output influence, with all terminal entropy retained. A034's available stage-1 report still proposes its two-row collision ledger as NEXT; no completed ledger is assumed. These elementary identities carry no novelty claim against other streams' broader collision work.

# A068 / B14 stage 4: parity-star geometry and a scoped collision payment

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let h be a regular Boolean sign function on q uniform bits, in the accepted recovered sense: after fixed orientations it is monotone with a total desirability order. Choose any vertex-disjoint stars on its coordinate set, with centers j and leaves L_j; unused vertices are allowed. Define physical data monomials

    y_j(x)=x_j product_{i in L_j} x_i for a center j,
    y_i(x)=x_i otherwise.

Let z contain m independent uniform physical bits, and let a_i(z)=(-1)^Q_i(z), where every Q_i has F2 degree at most two, with arbitrary overlaps. For every finite q,m, including constant controls, define G(x,z)=h(y_i(x)a_i(z)). This stage proves a complete normalized reference R with

    H(G)<=Gamma_R(G)<=C_reg I(h)+2I_z(G)
                       <=C_reg I_x(G)+2I_z(G)<=C_reg I(G),
    C_reg=log2(4+e)+2log2(e*pi)<8.937.

All costs are in bits; all influences refer to the stated physical coordinates. The rank-reference dependency is A068-QUADRATIC-CONTEXT-RANK-REFERENCE v1 with its accepted recovered C001 source. Only the ordinary influence bound is extended here, not the centered min(influence,1-influence) refinement.

The physical core after a star need not be unate. The example MAJ3(x1,x1*x2,x3) is nonunate and fails every fixed-order, fixed-orientation predecessor-antichain geometry. Thus this is a rank reference for a class beyond those physical one-order hypotheses. It is a narrow extension of the original packet, not a universal FEI claim.

We also independently reconstruct A079-SHARP-QUADRATIC-TWO-PHASE-COLLISION v1 at the same-full-physical-cube scope. This provides a genuine one-node C019 payment with both terminal phase entropies retained. It does not pay arbitrary unequal physical-cell collisions or deletion of the fresh data tag in our star geometry.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. An exact two-face influence reserve

For distinct core coordinates i,j, put

    P_ij=sum_{S containing i,j} hhat(S)^2.

The j-derivative d_j h has values in {0,sigma_j}, where sigma_j is its fixed unate sign. Fix all other coordinates and call its values at the two i endpoints a,b. Parseval on the derivative gives

    Inf_j(h)=E_rest[(a^2+b^2)/2],
    P_ij=E_rest[(a-b)^2/4],
    Inf_j(h)-2P_ij=E_rest[a b]>=0.

The equality identifies a physical reserve: the probability that j is pivotal at both i endpoints. It holds for every unate h, without regularity. Regularity is needed separately to pay the rank reference. For nonunate h, a b can be negative, so this argument cannot be iterated automatically after a shear.

### 2. Invertible physical labels and the complete rank reference

Use bit indicators for spectral subsets. Character substitution gives the label transform T(S):

    T_j=S_j for a star center j;
    T_i=S_i xor S_j for a leaf i of center j;
    T_i=S_i for unused vertices.

Since no center is itself a leaf and leaves belong to one center, applying the same transform again returns S. Thus it is a bijection on all 2^q labels, preserving the uniform data cube. Expanding the actual Boolean function gives

    G=sum_S hhat(S) chi_{T(S)}(x) a_S(z),
    a_S=product_{i in S} a_i,
    Ghat(T(S),U)=hhat(S) ahat_S(U).

There is no coherent sum at a full Fourier index: the data label, including the fresh parity tag, decodes S. Therefore

    H(G)=H(h)+sum_S hhat(S)^2 H(a_S),
    I_z(G)=sum_S hhat(S)^2 I(a_S).

For a quadratic phase a_S, its squared Walsh law is uniform on an affine flat L_S of dimension r_S=rank B_S. Autocorrelation is a character on ker B_S and zero elsewhere, so Fourier inversion proves this fact. At least r_S physical Fourier coordinates vary fairly on that flat, giving H(a_S)=r_S<=2I(a_S). This is physical mean degree, not an invariant-energy replacement.

Index the latent core coordinates by the accepted desirability order. Let w_k be the spectral level mass of h and Z_k=sum_{|S|=k} product_{i in S} i^-2. Define

    R(T(S),U)=w_|S| [product_{i in S} i^-2 / Z_|S|]
                    *2^-r_S *1_{U in L_S}.

For each latent S the affine factor sums to one, the rank factor sums to one on a level, and the level masses sum to one. T is bijective, hence the whole reference is normalized and covers the entire G spectrum. Its expected cost is precisely the same latent data rank/level cost as in accepted A068 v1, plus sum_S hhat(S)^2 r_S. The recovered bound pays the former by C_reg I(h); the physical phase inequality pays the latter by 2I_z(G). No level, rank normalizer, branch prior or terminal core entropy is dropped.

### 3. Payment in output influence

A leaf physical spectral bit changes from S_i to S_i xor S_j, giving

    Inf_{x_i}(G)=Inf_i(h)+Inf_j(h)-2P_ij.

A center's physical influence is Inf_j(h); unused coordinates also keep their core influence. Control packets sum to one, so the formulas are unchanged by overlapping quadratic controls. Consequently

    I_x(G)=I(h)+sum_j sum_{i in L_j}[Inf_j(h)-2P_ij]>=I(h).

Combined with the complete-reference bound above, this proves the stated all-q/all-m theorem. Disjointness of the star vertices is substantive: if a leaf collects several centers, its indicator is a higher parity and the pair reserve alone no longer controls its weight.

### 4. An actual function outside physical one-order geometry

Take h=MAJ3, center coordinate2 and leaf coordinate1. Then G=MAJ3(x1,x1*x2,x3). Its second derivative equals +1 at (x1,x3)=(+,-) and -1 at (-,+), so no fixed orientation makes G unate. A direct Walsh transform has four coefficients of magnitude1/2, entropy2 and influence3/2.

There is also a short proof that no physical order has all predecessor pivotal fibers antichains. If coordinate1 is last, its pivotal support contains both x3 endpoints at x2=+, hence a comparable pair in every orientation. If coordinate3 is last, both x1 endpoints at x2=- are pivotal, again comparable. Thus coordinate2 must be last. If coordinate1 is second, at x2=+ it is pivotal for both values of its predecessor x3; if coordinate3 is second, at x2=- it is pivotal for both predecessor x1 values. Both cases fail. Exact enumeration of all6 orders and8 fixed orientations supplies explicit failing fibers in out/geometry.json. Even choosing an orientation separately on these failing full one-dimensional fibers cannot make them antichains.

The proof uses the invertible latent geometry and its two-face reserve to pay the rank reference. It does not silently reuse a physical predecessor-antichain estimate that the example violates.

### 5. Independent reconstruction of A079's actual two-phase collision

Let g0,g1 be actual quadratic signs on the same full physical n-bit cube and F(t,z)=g_t(z). Put a=(I(g0)+I(g1))/2, mu=E(g0g1), d=(1-mu)/2 and L=H(F)-(H(g0)+H(g1))/2. Direct Walsh transformation gives parent coefficients (ghat0(U)+ghat1(U))/2 and (ghat0(U)-ghat1(U))/2. The complete spectral law is normalized by Parseval, I(F)=a+d, and both terminal entropies remain in L's definition.

Write child affine Fourier support sizes M and RM, with R>=1 a power of4, and let u be the fraction of the smaller support in the intersection. Each intersection coefficient has the two masses (1+R^-1/2)^2/(4M) and (1-R^-1/2)^2/(4M). Summing their entropy and the two equal masses on every nonintersection atom independently yields

    L=2-u gamma_R,
    gamma_1=2,
    gamma_4=(9/8)log2(3)-1,
    gamma_16=(25/32)log2(5)+(9/32)log2(3)-2.

At any R, setting t=R^-1/2 gives
gamma_R=1+1/R+[(1+t)^2/4]log2[(1+t)^2/4]+[(1-t)^2/4]log2[(1-t)^2/4]+log2(R)/(2R).
Also L<=2 by the one-bit mixing entropy plus one-bit conditional selector entropy. Parseval gives |mu|<=u/sqrt(R).

If r_D is the polar rank of g0g1, then mu is zero or has magnitude2^-r_D/2, and |r0-r1|<=r_D<=r0+r1. For mu<=0, d>=1/2 and L<=4d. For mu=1, L=d=0. If mu=1/2, R=1 or4: R1 gives L<=4d; R4 forces u=1 and L=kappa/4, where kappa=12-(9/2)log2(3). If mu=1/4, R=1,4,16: respectively L/d<=4, L/d<=(8+kappa)/3<kappa, or L/d=(8/3)L16<75/16<kappa, with L16=4-(25/32)log2(5)-(9/32)log2(3)<225/128. Smaller positive mu gives d>=7/16 and L/d<=32/7<kappa. These exhaust all dimensions and prove L<=kappa d.

For the sharp whole bound, multiplying F by the selector character preserves entropy and a and reduces negative mu to |mu|. Each child pays entropy r_i<=2I(g_i), so H(F)<=2a+L. The ratio (2a+b)/(a+d) is bounded at the allowed minimum a or by its limit2, because its derivative has sign2d-b. At mu=1/2,R4 the minimum a=1/2 gives Cstar=(4+kappa)/3=16/3-(3/2)log2(3). The remaining rank cases give upper bounds12/5 (mu1/2,R1), 28/11 (mu1/4,R1), (32+kappa)/15 (mu1/4,R4), 481/176 (mu1/4,R16), and80/31 (smaller positive mu), all below Cstar.

At mu=0, rank sum>=4 gives8/3. Rank sum0 gives two disjoint characters, entropy2 and a>=1/2, hence ratio<=2. Rank sum2 gives one character and a disjoint affine plane, entropy3. If the character is nonempty then a>=1. Otherwise the plane excludes the origin: it has a fixed-one coordinate or at least three varying physical coordinates, so its mean weight is at least3/2 and a>=3/4. The ratio is then<=12/5. The |mu|=1 case is a single quadratic phase, possibly times the selector, with ratio<=2. Integer comparisons 3^5<2^8, 3^12>2^19 and5^10>2^23 imply 24/5<kappa<39/8, Cstar>44/15 and the L16 bound used above.

Equality is attained by g0=1, g1=(-1)^(z1*z2): a=1/2,d=1/4,H(F)=4-(9/8)log2(3). Thus the source theorem and constants hold at stated scope, with no repair supplied. With X=selector,Y=(z1,z2), its conditional entropy is2-(3/8)log2(3), exceeding2I_Y=1. The admitted distinct-label source (-1)^(x*t+t*z1*z2) restricts at x=0 to this actual witness. This is a real Boolean loss of conditional constant2, not an arbitrary unnormalized phase array.

The resulting certified C019 node payment is H(F)=L+(r0+r1)/2<=kappa d+2a. The sharper Cstar constant spends terminal rank slack once; it cannot be reused at descendants without a new argument. Here arbitrary unequal Fourier support sizes are covered; unequal physical partition cells and repeated collisions are not.

### 6. The precise remaining gap

A one-leaf star retains a fresh physical tag v: the two votes are x and x*v. Their spectral labels decode the two original votes. Fixing v merges the labels and creates exactly the signed two-row coefficients from stage3. The parent influence need not survive that deletion, and the inherited reference can cost one bit at zero output influence in the AND2 witness. The two-face reserve above prices retaining v, not deleting it.

First unsupported line: after deleting the tag with arbitrary nonconstant terminal cores, a cancellation-aware normalized output reference has cost paid by output influence using a single unused reserve. A079 handles two full-cube unit quadratic phases, not the unequal-amplitude regular-core packets of a general deletion. No uniform arbitrary collision closure, iterated-star closure, universal C018/C019/C020 payment or Boolean counterexample to FEI is claimed.

# A068 / B14 stage5: actual tag deletion and a paid strong-pair geometry

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

This run closes two scoped cases of actual data identification, retaining complete output references. All functions are Boolean signs on uniform independent physical bits, entropy and reference costs in bits.

(A) For every threshold core h(t)=+1 iff sum_i w_i t_i>=theta, any finite number of arbitrary overlapping quadratic sign controls a_i(z), and a_2=sigma*a_1 with constant sigma=+1 or-1, identify t's physical data inputs by x2=epsilon*x1. Both epsilon cofactors admit a normalized rank/affine reference with

    H(F_epsilon)<=Gamma_epsilon<=C_reg I_data(F_epsilon)+2I_controls(F_epsilon).

This is an exact closure/application of accepted A068 v1 after recompiling the threshold. It is not claimed as a new independently defined FEI class. Zero and negative merged weights are included; parent influence is not substituted for output influence.

(B) Let g be any accepted regular core on private weak data y, a and b_i arbitrary quadratic controls on independent physical U,Z, and beta any quadratic sign depending only on U. Take U to be exactly beta's active physical coordinates; inactive coordinates belong to Z. After identifying the two strong inputs in the regular core Maj3(t1,t2,g(weak)), the actual output is

    F(x,y,U,Z)=Maj3(x*a(U,Z), x*a(U,Z)*beta(U), g(y_i*b_i(U,Z))).

For every finite dimension, including constant beta (empty U), this output admits a normalized complete reference satisfying

    H(F)<=Gamma_F<=C_reg I_{x,y}(F)+2I_Z(F)+8I_U(F)<=C_reg I(F),
    C_reg=log2(4+e)+2log2(e*pi)>8, C_reg<8.937.

There is no fresh parity tag in F. The shared controllers may depend on every U and Z coordinate. The hypothesis is that the aligned strong pair selects a balanced private character, and the opposite pair selects a core independent of private x. A weak or unequal pair need not have that contrast.

For any Boolean core, not only thresholds, the common-phase deletion also has an exact phase-neutral entropy identity proved below. No arbitrary-control FEI statement follows from that identity.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. The signed common-phase merger is phase-neutral

Write the physical tagged star as G(x,v,rest,z)=h(x*a1(z),x*v*a2(z),rest controls), with a2=sigma*a1. Fix a subset R of all other core coordinates, and put

    A=hhat(R), B=hhat(R+1), C=hhat(R+2), D=hhat(R+1+2).

The tag cofactor at v=epsilon has two data packets, with core amplitudes A+epsilon*sigma*D and B+epsilon*sigma*C. The first carries control phase a_R; the second carries a_R*a1. These are genuine signed sums before squaring. Within each paired parent packet the phase is identical up to its constant sigma sign. Therefore averaging the two cofactors uses

    [(A+D)^2+(A-D)^2]/2=A^2+D^2,

and similarly for B,C. Every conditional control-phase entropy term is conserved on average. If h_epsilon(t,rest)=h(t,epsilon*sigma*t,rest), then exactly

    H(G)-[H(G_+)+H(G_-)]/2
       =H(h)-[H(h_+)+H(h_-)]/2,
    Inf_v(G)=Inf_2(h).

The data-label bijection before restriction preserves H(h); afterward each cofactor has distinct remaining labels. Conditional data signs are uniform for every controller assignment, proving the influence identity. These arguments permit arbitrary Boolean control signs; no FEI claim for those signs is made. For quadratic controls, their flat-fiber reference cost is their entropy rank, so that part of the complete cost is likewise conserved on average. All core entropy remains; this identity alone does not price an arbitrary core decrement.

### 2. Recompile threshold cofactors before making the reference

In (A), substitute x2=epsilon*x1 directly into the threshold sum. The merged coefficient is

    w'_1=w1+epsilon*sigma*w2.

Every other coefficient and theta stays fixed. Delete zero coefficients, absorb signs of remaining coefficients into constant flips of their quadratic controls, and reorder their absolute weights. The result is a regular threshold core on distinct remaining physical data coordinates. Constant outputs are handled with the point-mass reference on the empty Fourier set; unused coordinates have zero Fourier label.

Apply accepted A068 v1 to this compiled core. Its normalized data-level/Euler-rank reference times quadratic affine-flat factors gives exactly the bound in (A), using the actual output's influences. Neither a parent influence nor a parent rank charge remains. If all coefficients cancel, the entire reference cost is zero. Both nonconstant terminal core spectra remain in the nonconstant cases; the proof does not replace them with point masses.

The inherited parent pushforward need not have this property: the stage3 AND2 witness retains one bit of inherited cost at zero output influence. Here the final reference is rebuilt from the output geometry, not asserted to be the inherited reference with a free reset. The equality/constant-relative-phase condition is substantive; this does not assert general reference-reset payment on arbitrary children.

### 3. A protected actual collision with arbitrary quadratic relative phase

In (B), the outer majority selects x*a when beta=+1 and selects the weak core when beta=-1. Its original core Maj3(t1,t2,g) is regular whenever g is regular: the two strong coordinates are equally most desirable; they dominate every weak coordinate, and the weak coordinates inherit g's desirability chain. Fixed weak orientations can be incorporated in their controls. Thus (B) is an actual single data identification from the admitted parent class, not a fabricated Fourier array.

Let a=|U|. For every active coordinate j of a quadratic beta, its F2 derivative polynomial is a nonzero affine function. Its physical influence is therefore1/2 or1. Consequently

    I_U(beta)>=a/2.

On every physical U-edge crossing beta, one output endpoint is x times a fixed control sign and the other is a weak-core value independent of private x. Conditional on all other bits, they disagree for exactly one of the two x values. Shared controller dependence changes neither fact. Noncrossing edges can only add influence, so

    Inf_{U_j}(F)>=Inf_j(beta)/2,
    I_U(F)>=a/4.

This is the one physical budget paying the decoder. It does not use the parent influence or an independent copy of terminal influence. Constant beta has a=0 and is one admitted leaf.

### 4. Complete block reference, with all terminal debts retained

Resolve physical U. For each u in its 2^a assignments, the restricted control phases on Z are still quadratic. The actual leaf F_u is either x*a_u(Z), or g(y_i*b_{i,u}(Z)). Give the first its character/affine-flat reference, of cost<=2I_Z(F_u), and the second its accepted full rank reference, of cost<=C_reg I_{x,y}(F_u)+2I_Z(F_u). In both cases a common valid bound is that latter expression. Extend references by zero on unused data coordinates and call them Q_u(T), normalized on the entire remaining spectrum.

Define

    Qbar(T)=2^-a sum_u Q_u(T),
    R(A,T)=2^-a Qbar(T) for every spectral U-label A.

Both distributions are normalized. Block Walsh Parseval gives the actual rest marginal P_rest(T)=2^-a sum_u Fhat_u(T)^2. R covers every parent coefficient because a nonzero coefficient must arise from a nonzero leaf coefficient. Hence the entire cost, including spectral block labels and branch prior, obeys

    Gamma_R(F)=a+E_{P_rest} log2(1/Qbar)
               <=2a+2^-a sum_u Gamma_{Q_u}(F_u).

The second a is explicitly paid via Qbar>=2^-a Q_u. Signed interference across leaves is retained by the Walsh identity, not replaced by a false full-spectrum mixture. Physical influences outside U commute with this restriction average, giving

    avg_u I_{x,y}(F_u)=I_{x,y}(F),
    avg_u I_Z(F_u)=I_Z(F).

Thus Gamma_R(F)<=2a+C_reg I_{x,y}+2I_Z. The protected edge inequality pays2a<=8I_U and proves (B). C_reg>8 follows, for example, from (4+e)e^2*pi^2>6*7*9>256. This proof works for any number of active quadratic relative-phase coordinates; resolving many coordinates does not leave a dimension-sized unpaid bill.

The separate common-phase strong-pair tag node has leaves x*a and the complete weak core. Their data spectra are disjoint (one contains private x, the other never does), even with shared controllers. Its exact entropy decrement is2 and tag influence1/2, so the recovered orthogonal C019 node price is4Inf_tag, with both terminal entropies retained. This is located at our actual deletion interface, not claimed as a new orthogonality theorem.

### 5. What the new A079 dependency does and does not cover

Every conditional row must be a coordinate product before the single-nonfair theorem can be invoked. Our actual five-bit threshold output from weights(3,2,2,1),theta1, tag=-1 and control flags(0,0,2), with the first two data coordinates as physical address block, has row-J=0 squared raw coefficients

    (64,0,0,64,0,0,0,0).

Their total is128, marginals are(64,64,0), and a flattening has matrix[[64,0],[0,64]], determinant4096. All varying marginals are fair, yet the joint row is not a product. Its normalized law has two atoms at000 and011; a product would have four atoms. This is an actual Boolean scope obstruction, not a refutation of A079. Another address block is not ruled out by this certificate. In the declared census,3616 canonical-address outputs fail the admitted row hypothesis; no whole-theorem A079 acceptance or quantitative substitution is inferred from those failures.

### 6. The exact remaining line

For a weak or unequal identified pair and a nonconstant relative quadratic phase, the merged threshold coefficient depends on controls. A fixed recompiled core is unavailable, and neither branch need be a balanced private character independent of the other branch's core. A relative-phase crossing can then have arbitrarily small conditional disagreement. The bound I_U>=a/4 and the uniform2a-bit decoder payment are unsupported there.

First unsupported universal line: replace that protected contrast by a signed conditional-packet/terminal-slack payment for two nonconstant overlapping cofactors, retaining a normalized final reference and spending one unused physical account. Nor is (B)'s private-arm condition automatically preserved after x is queried. The finite AND3 exposure below has I_U drop from1/2 to1/8 in its x=- child; this is a boundary fixture, not a new infinite no-go (A070 already supplies that obstruction type). The claims prove scoped closures, not hereditary full FEI or an unbounded H/I family.
