# A069 / B14: stable transfer of the percolation mask decoder

Full FEI remains OPEN. This is a complete local derivation for a proper family and an abstract perturbation interface; independent acceptance and supervisor copying are pending. All entropy is in bits.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let g_d be complete depth-d ternary recursive majority on n=3^d independent uniform sign bits, d>=1. For any N>=n, pad g_d with N-n unused bits. For an arbitrary deterministic edit set D in the N-cube, let f=g_d off D and f=-g_d on D, and rho=|D|/2^N. The target proved here is a complete, injective prefix code for every spectral subset, including the empty subset, with:

* H(f)<=E_{S_f} L_T(S_f)<=12 I(f) whenever N rho<=1/8.
* For a single edited input, H(f)<=E_{S_f} L_T(S_f)<=25 I(f), uniformly in d,N and edit location; the constant 12 applies for N>=6.
* Its cost is stable under padding followed by one point flip: for fixed d, the cost difference from the padded base code tends to zero as N tends to infinity.

The abstract hypothesis is a fixed rooted partition tree T with singleton leaves, at most three children per internal vertex, and a paid spectral occupancy sum. Its coordinates and child order are fixed before the sample is drawn. No spatial estimate is assumed. Tree choice may depend on the function, but not on the realized Fourier set.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Execution protocol: fei-execution-protocol/2, revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2, all rules read. Campaign README, current status, claim/obstruction registries, A069 packet entry, stream actions, campaign plan B14/A069 acceptance contract, supplemental CAMPAIGN/CLAIMS/COUNTEREXAMPLES/SEED_LEMMAS/NATIVE_MAPPING were consulted. BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 22:37 UTC and B14 STREAM_NOTES.md v1, 2026-10-11 22:48 UTC supplied the nonduplication and padding-flip directions.

Primary local mathematical texts: percolation_spectral_pivotal_entropy_30pp.txt, Lemma 2.1 (refinement decoder), Lemma 4.1 (block variance), Proposition 4.3 (occupancy payment), Theorem 4.4 (arm criterion). The 31-page other version was compared at its abstract and corresponding lemma/theorem locators. threshold_paper_38pp.txt, Section 7, Proposition 7.1 and Corollaries 7.2-7.3 give disjoint composition and the existing read-once baseline; the earlier 29-page version was compared at its introduction and Section 7 locators. Supplemental BLOCK_RESTRICTION_IDENTITY_2026-09-18.md and prior B05/B19 reports were consulted for scope. Source hashes and precise paths are in out/source_manifest.json. No web literature/priority search or new prior-art claim is made.

The source does NOT say that a spectral sample is a deterministic function of a pivotal set, or of a few arms. It decodes the sample from occupied-cell masks. Arm events bound block oscillation probabilities, which bound spectral occupancy. These two steps must be distinguished.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### 1. Abstract all-set decoder and complete cost

For each internal vertex v, let C_v be its cell and b_v its number of children, with 2<=b_v<=3. Encode the empty set by the one-bit word 0. Encode a nonempty set by 1 and, in preorder, encode every occupied internal vertex's nonempty child mask using b_v bits. Number masks 1,...,2^{b_v}-1 and write mask-1 in b_v bits. One word is unused at each vertex. Unoccupied vertices are skipped. At singleton leaves, occupation already identifies the coordinate, so no further bits are needed.

The decoder knows which occupied vertices remain from previously decoded masks. It stops precisely when this list is empty. This proves unique decoding, termination, and prefix freedom. Every subset has a word. Thus Q_T(S)=2^{-L_T(S)} has total mass <=1 by Kraft, with

    L_T(S)=1 + sum_{v internal} b_v 1{S intersects C_v}.

The root bit, all child choices, and all physical coordinate locations are paid: locations are specified by the fixed tree and the decoded masks. There are no latent witnesses, unrevealed leaves, sample-dependent geometry choices, or omitted reference normalizers. The fixed decoder is available for all sets, including those absent from g's spectrum. Gibbs' inequality for the subprobability Q_T gives

    H(f)<=Gamma_T(f):=sum_S fhat(S)^2 L_T(S)
         =1+sum_v b_v P(S_f intersects C_v).                 (1)

Indeed normalize Q_T by its mass Z<=1, use KL>=0, and note that adding log2 Z<=0 can only reduce the cross-entropy bound. This is the integral-bit version of percolation Lemma 2.1. Its abstract influence hypothesis is Gamma_T(f)<=K I(f). Decoding alone never proves that hypothesis.

A tree without unary vertices has at most N-1 internal vertices, since sum_v(b_v-1)=N-1. Therefore

    max_S L_T(S)<=1+3(N-1)=3N-2.                           (2)

### 2. Perturbation transfer, valid without spatial or Boolean spectral assumptions

For Boolean f,g on the same uniform N-cube, put delta=f-g and rho=P(f!=g), so ||delta||_2^2=4rho. For any fixed nonnegative finite cost L, weighted Euclidean Minkowski gives

    sqrt(Gamma_L(f)) <= sqrt(Gamma_L(g))
                        +sqrt(sum_S deltahat(S)^2 L(S))
                     <= sqrt(Gamma_L(g))+2 sqrt(rho max L). (3)

Equivalently, |Gamma_L(f)-Gamma_L(g)| is at most

    4 sqrt(rho max L Gamma_L(g)) + 4rho max L.              (4)

For linear payment one may use (a+b)^2<=2a^2+2b^2 coefficientwise:

    Gamma_T(f)<=2Gamma_T(g)+8rho(3N-2).                    (5)

This retains coherent cross terms; it does not treat squared spectra as an incoherent mixture. The only Boolean input here is ||f-g||_2^2=4rho. The weighted triangle statement itself holds for arbitrary real vectors.

The physical edge events for f and g can differ only if x or x with coordinate i flipped lies in D. Thus

    |Inf_i(f)-Inf_i(g)|<=2rho,
    |I(f)-I(g)|<=2N rho.                                  (6)

For a one-point edit at x0, deltahat(S)=-2g(x0)2^{-N} chi_S(x0) for every S. In particular, for any cell C,

    sum_{S intersects C} deltahat(S)^2
       =4*2^{-N}(1-2^{-|C|}),
    P(S_f intersects C)<=2P(S_g intersects C)
                         +8*2^{-N}(1-2^{-|C|}).           (7)

This directly exhibits stability of the occupancy sum itself, even though the edited Fourier support can become dense.

### 3. Direct nongeometric payment for recursive majority

The three-bit majority gate has the exact Boolean expansion

    Maj3(y1,y2,y3)=(y1+y2+y3-y1*y2*y3)/2.

Its spectral child mask is one of {1},{2},{3},{1,2,3}, each with probability 1/4. At every composition node the child functions are balanced and use disjoint physical blocks. Consequently any output Fourier set has a unique decomposition into its occupied child blocks; substitution multiplies their coefficients with no interference. Conditional on the child mask, their nonempty spectral samples are independent copies of the child law. This follows directly from the expansion and agrees with threshold Proposition 7.1.

An individual child is selected with probability 1/2. Thus any main-tree cell at depth j is occupied with probability 2^{-j}. There are 3^j such cells, so the expected number occupied at that level is (3/2)^j. Singleton leaves yield

    I(g_d)=(3/2)^d,
    E(number of occupied main internal nodes)
       =sum_{j=0}^{d-1}(3/2)^j=2(I(g_d)-1).               (8)

This is the required occupancy growth/payment replacing percolation's arm estimates. It is proved from the named Boolean family, not imported from spatial geometry. The four-mask two-bit code on the unedited support also gives the known identity H(g_d)=4(I(g_d)-1). That support-only code is NOT used after editing.

To code all subsets, use the three-bit masks of section 1. If there are padding bits, attach the main ternary tree and an arbitrary binary tree on all padding bits to a new binary root. Under g_d the main branch is always occupied and the padding branch never is. Write Ig=I(g_d). Exactly,

    Gamma_T(g_d)=1+6(Ig-1)+2*1{N>3^d} <=6Ig-3.            (9)

Combining (5), (6), and (9), for every edit set D,

    H(f)<=Gamma_T(f)
         <=12Ig-6+8rho(3N-2)
         <=12I(f)-6+48N rho-16rho.                        (10)

Hence N rho<=1/8 implies Gamma_T(f)<=12I(f). This proves the sparse-edit theorem for arbitrary edit sets, including highly irregular, nonmonotone edits and all choices of signs inherited from g_d.

For a single point, rho=2^{-N}, N>=3. Since N/2^N<=3/8, (10) gives Gamma_T(f)<=12I(f)+12. Balance of g_d gives Ef=+-2^{1-N}, hence Var(f)=1-4*2^{-2N}>=15/16. Parseval gives I(f)>=Var(f), and consequently

    Gamma_T(f)<= (12+12/(15/16))I(f)
              =(124/5)I(f)<25I(f).                       (11)

For N>=6, N/2^N<=1/8, so the sharper constant 12 follows from (10). No floating-point logarithmic estimate supplies any of these signs.

### 4. Padding stability and scope controls

For fixed d, Gamma_T(g_d) is constant for N>3^d by (9). By (2) and (4), a one-point edit changes that paid cost by at most

    4 sqrt(2^{-N}(3N-2)Gamma_T(g_d))
       +4*2^{-N}(3N-2),                                  (12)

which tends to zero. Influence convergence follows from (6). For completeness, spectral total variation tau<=2 sqrt(2^{-N}) follows by Cauchy-Schwarz applied to (fhat-ghat)(fhat+ghat). A maximal coupling and the conditional entropy chain rule give |H(f)-H(g_d)|<=h2(tau)+tau log2(2^N-1), which also tends to zero. Thus this functional passes the B14 perturbation test in its actual paid domain.

The base recursive-majority family is already covered by read-once composition (threshold Corollary 7.3 and the cited composition literature). Reproving its FEI is NOT a new class advance. The additional deliverable is an all-set hierarchy that remains paid after arbitrary sparse truth-table edits; edits are not assumed to preserve the original read-once representation. We do not claim that this neighborhood theorem has no prior literature overlap. For example, changing Maj3 at the all-positive input makes it non-unate: from that input a single negative input changes output -1 to +1, whereas elsewhere the same coordinate has the original positive majority direction. This edited example lies outside the unate/desirability hypothesis of C001. We make no assertion that every edited member is outside every structured FEI theorem.

The normalized non-Boolean control a(x)=N^{-1/2} sum_i x_i has spectrum uniform on singleton sets and I(a)=1. At N=3^d, the same complete ternary tree has one expected occupied vertex at each internal depth, giving Gamma_T(a)=1+3d. This diverges. The decoder and stability lemmas remain valid; the Boolean-family occupancy payment (8) is the indispensable extra step. There is no extension of (8) from normalization alone. An arbitrary tensor factor is not allowed as an unpaid terminal in the family theorem.

First unsupported universal line: for every Boolean f, choose a bounded-arity fixed partition tree, or a fixed-prior collection of complete tree references, whose full expected code cost is <=K I(f) for one absolute K. This report proves that choice/payment only for the recursive-majority neighborhood above. It neither proves nor disproves that universal statement.

# A069 stage 2: aligned edit energy and the addressing hierarchy reference

Full FEI remains OPEN. The original A069 v1 theorem has now been independently accepted by A070 at its stated scope. This stage supplies one bundled scoped extension, awaiting independent review.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Uniform Boolean sign functions, Fourier probabilities fhat(S)^2, entropy and code lengths in bits, physical influence I=sum |S| fhat(S)^2. A hierarchy reference uses a rooted partition tree fixed before observing S, singleton leaves and bounded arity. Its complete all-set code is L_T(S)=1+sum_v b_v 1{S intersects C_v}, with Gamma_T(f)=E L_T(S_f) and H(f)<=Gamma_T(f).

New paid family: ordinary a-bit addressing, a>=1, on n=a+2^a bits padded to arbitrary N>=n, with arbitrary truth-table edits of density rho. The explicit binary tree below satisfies Gamma_T(f)<=16I(f) if N*rho<=1/8; every one-point edit satisfies Gamma_T(f)<=25I(f), and the 16 bound applies for N>=6. This is a complete code, not merely an entropy estimate inherited from unedited addressing.

New generic interface: for any Boolean g and any nonempty proper physical block Z, flip g on the subcube z=z*. Derive the exact weighted edit-spectrum cost in a fixed tree whose root separates Z and its complement Y. Quantify the retained restricted-core term and its physical-Parseval bound. No universal FEI conclusion for this interface is asserted.

Lead-directed calibration: Gamma(g_2)=17/2 at N=9 and 21/2 at N=10,11,12. Reuse the already certified 512 N=9 one-point edits with bound 25I, and the N=10 base; directly check the new N=11,12 bases.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Stage-start BOARD.md supervisor snapshot 2026-10-12 04:39 UTC, B14 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 (2026-10-11 23:25 UTC), own STATUS and stage-1 claims/output were read. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md contains the complete finite-sum proof, equations (A1)-(A4) and (S1)-(S5). The critical steps are reproduced here.

Addressing has one uniformly chosen data coordinate j and an independently uniform subset of its a selector coordinates in every spectral sample. Use a balanced binary tree on selector coordinates, a complete binary tree on the 2^a data coordinates, and a binary root joining them. Add a binary padding tree under a second join if N>n. Define R(1)=0 and R(k)=1-2^{-k}+R(floor(k/2))+R(ceil(k/2)) for k>=2. An address cell of size k is occupied with probability 1-2^{-k}; the data sample occupies one path of a internal vertices. Consequently

    Gamma_T(A_a)=3+2a+2R(a)+2*1{N>n}<=8I(A_a)-5.

The decoder covers all subsets, including tails created by editing. Its maximum length is 2N-1. The accepted weighted-L2 edit bound and |I(f)-I(A_a)|<=2N*rho give

    Gamma_T(f)<=16I(f)-10+48N*rho-8rho.

Thus N*rho<=1/8 closes the 16I bound. For a single edit, N*2^{-N}<=3/8 and I(f)>=Var(f)>=15/16 because addressing is balanced. Hence Gamma_T(f)<=16I(f)+8<25I(f). No omitted label, empty atom, reference normalizer, or terminal entropy remains. For fixed a, the weighted-L2 cost difference under padding and a single flip tends to zero.

The influence reference instead costs exactly

    log2(e)+a+(a/2)*log2(a+2)+log2((a+2)/2)

on unedited (and padded) addressing. Its ratio to I is log2(a)+2+o(1), whereas the hierarchy ratio is <=8. This comparison locates the gain: a whole spectral set shares a single data path and the selector masks. Unedited addressing's FEI was already proved; this is a complete edit-stable reference, not a new claim to that baseline.

For the subcube interface, let rho=2^{-r}, h=g restricted to z=z*, m_h=Eh, and delta=f-g=-2*1{z=z*}h. Let W_Z,W_Y be the internal-mask costs of the two binary subtrees, and U_Z=E_{uniform subset of Z}W_Z=sum_v 2*(1-2^{-|C_v|}). Direct coefficient factorization gives the exact identity

    E_delta:=sum_S deltahat(S)^2 L_T(S)
       =4rho*[1+2*(1-rho*m_h^2)+U_Z+E_{S_h}W_Y].

The empty coefficient is included. The last term is the restricted core's actual spectrum, not an assumed low-cost Boolean leaf. Partial Parseval gives E_{S_g}W_Y=E_z E_{S_{g_z}}W_Y, so rho*E_{S_h}W_Y<=E_{S_g}W_Y. Since U_Z<=2(r-1),

    E_delta<=4E_{S_g}W_Y+4rho*(2r+1),
    Gamma_T(f)<=2Gamma_T(g)+8E_{S_g}W_Y+8rho*(2r+1).

This replaces the crude rho*N charge by an explicit control-size charge plus a source-compatible retained-core charge. On every free-coordinate edge, editing both endpoints leaves pivotality unchanged. On each control-coordinate edge crossing the subcube boundary, exactly one of g,f is pivotal. Thus Inf_i(f)=Inf_i(g) for i in Y and I_Z(f)+I_Z(g)>=2r*rho. The weighted triangle handles g/delta interference; squared spectra of g and f are never replaced by an incoherent mixture.

First unsupported universal line: choose a complete paid separating source reference for arbitrary Boolean g and transport its retained-core cost with a single influence budget that survives cancellation to f. The exact subcube identities do not prove that line. In particular an edit can make f constant, while the root-bit code still costs one, so no all-subcube Gamma_T(f)<=C I(f) claim is made.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md equations (1)-(10) contain the complete proof. For each internal cell C_v, couple X with X^(v) by independently resampling that cell while retaining all coordinates outside it. For a Boolean sign u let D_v(u)=1{u(X)!=u(X^(v))}. Conditional independent copies and Parseval give

    W_T(u)=2 sum_v b_v P(D_v(u)=1).

Disagreements of a Boolean product combine by XOR. For two signs u,w,

    W_T(uw)=W_T(u)+W_T(w)-4 sum_v b_v P(D_v(u)=D_v(w)=1).

Thus Gamma_T(uw)<=Gamma_T(u)+Gamma_T(w)-1 even with shared physical variables. This is code-cost subadditivity, not the false general Fourier-entropy subadditivity for overlapping products.

For three signs g,q1,q2, let M_v=D_v(g)+D_v(q1)+D_v(q2). Then M_v minus its parity is 2 if M_v>=2 and zero otherwise. Consequently

    Gamma_T(g*q1*q2)=Gamma_T(g)+W_T(q1)+W_T(q2)-R3,
    R3=4 sum_v b_v P(M_v>=2)>=0.

This is one simultaneous refund event for each resampled cell pair, not separate payments for each Fourier pairing. Equivalently R3=R_T(q1,q2)+R_T(g,q1*q2); these two events are disjoint on every resampled pair. The ledger retains positive edit/edit cross terms, source/edit rebates, and the intersection correction through actual Boolean multiplication.

For the compatible-overlap family, let A={C=c*,U=u*}, B={C=c*,V=v*}, q1=1-2*1_A, q2=1-2*1_B. Choose binary block trees and T=(T_Y,(T_C,(T_U,T_V))). A codimension-k subcube mask of density rho has occupation probability 4rho*(1-2^{-|E intersects its fixed coordinates|}) in any cell E, and I=2k*rho. Each event occupies at most three join cells, its common block tree and one exclusive block tree. Hence W_T(qj)<=6I(qj), uniformly in block sizes, with no core-coordinate label charge.

Write c=2^{-r}, a=2^{-s}, b=2^{-t}. Direct physical pivotal counts give

    I(q1*q2)=2c[sa+tb+r(a+b-2ab)],
    I(q1)+I(q2)=2c[sa+tb+r(a+b)].

Since a,b<=1/2, their sum is at most twice the net controller influence. The exact refund then gives

    H(f)<=Gamma_T(f)<=Gamma_T(g)+12I(q1*q2).

The source term is not dropped or renewed in the two edits. The complete code is fixed jointly for the two events. Constants, core means and empty spectral atoms are allowed in the ledger. For g=h(Y), disjoint physical product gives I(f)=I(h)+I(q1*q2), and Gamma_T(g)=Gamma_(T_Y)(h)+2Var(h). If the retained core already has Gamma_(T_Y)(h)<=K I(h), spectral Poincare Var(h)<=I(h) yields H(f)<=max(K+2,12)I(f). This conditional product consequence is not a new literature novelty claim; the shared-variable source-cost identity is the transfer mechanism under study.

The A070 witness g=x1 on two bits, edits (+,+) and (-,+), still has positive weighted difference cross +1/2 with zero edit intersection. The new ledger gives W(g)=W(f)=2, input-mask costs 3/2 each, and simultaneous refund R3=3. It therefore reproduces Gamma(f)=Gamma(g)=3 without using a physical-overlap-only cross bound. Sequential overlap is checked at the level f-g=e1+e2-2e12 before squaring.

First unsupported universal line: for g with controller dependence, use the exact refund to transport a paid source cost and net-controller cost to I(g*q1*q2), while retaining all conditional core costs under one physical influence account. Cancellation can make f constant when g=q1*q2. Also the input-mask-to-net-influence comparison requires the private-block hypotheses; identical masks cancel to the constant mask and defeat such a comparison. Nothing here proves a universal paid source hierarchy or a C019 physical first-query stopping rule. The ownership proved is within cell-resampling experiments, not ownership of a physical restriction-tree budget.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

DERIVATION.md supplies the entire proof, equations(1)-(11). Exactly cancel q1 before selecting the final output reference. # A071 / B15: bounded search-space design and exact biased-block grid certificate

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Target: B09-BIASED-BLOCK-UNCERTAINTY@1 in CLAIM_REGISTRY.json. For any actual Boolean sign under independent interior biases and a physical block A with complement B, the live helper asserts 0 <= J_A <= M_A+Delta_A. Here M_A=H(S_A|S_B), J_A=H(S_B)-E_Z H(mu_Z), and Delta_A=sum_i_in_A[h2(p_i)+log2(max(p_i,1-p_i))]. All entropy is in bits. Z is a physical input assignment, not a spectral conditioning event. We test its upper inequality; J>=0 follows from the exact mixture identity and entropy concavity.

Family: all three-bit truth tables, all six nonempty proper physical blocks, and p_i in {1/8,1/4,1/2,3/4,7/8}. Assignments x=0..7 use little-endian bits. Output complement lets f(000)=+1; seven code bits specify the remaining signs, giving 128 tables. Input flips paired with p_i -> 1-p_i preserve every squared coefficient (a sign changes on the flipped basis coordinate) and biject the physical children with unchanged branch weights. Delta also stays fixed. Therefore use ordered p_i in {1/8,1/4,1/2}: 27 bias vectors. This covers the entire raw 256*125*6=192000 grid with 128*27*6=20736 representatives. It is a covering reduction, not a claim that all representatives lie in distinct orbits. No coordinate permutations or multiplication by an input character are quotiented: biased character multiplication does not preserve squared spectra generally.

Dummy coordinates embed all n<=2 proper-block cases. Empty blocks have J=M=Delta=0; full blocks have J=0 and M>=0, Delta>=0. For p<=1/2, delta=p log2((1-p)/p)>=0, and reflection handles p>=1/2. No claim covers off-grid parameters or n>=4. A finite helper falsifier would be sufficient to refute its universal statement, but this search found none.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Let m_i=8p_i, w(x)=product_i[m_i if x_i=1 else 8-m_i], and V_S=sum_x f(x)w(x) product_i_in_S(8x_i-m_i). In the orthonormal centered basis phi_i=(x_i-p_i)/sqrt(p_i(1-p_i)), the exact squared coefficient is

    mu(S) = V_S^2 / [8^(2n) product_i_in_S m_i(8-m_i)].

This follows by writing each expectation with its physical probability numerator and cancelling the 8 powers in the variance normalization. All masses are rational, nonnegative and sum to one (checked for every parent and child). Each restricted table is constructed by the actual fixed physical assignment. The code checks q(T)=sum_R mu(R union T)=sum_z P(Z=z)mu_z(T) in Fraction arithmetic for every tested case. Thus the defect is exactly

    D = M+Delta-J = H(mu)-2H(q)+E_Z H(mu_Z)+Delta.

For rational t in [1,2], use y=(t-1)/(t+1), so 0<=y<=1/3. The natural logarithm has positive expansion 2 sum_j>=0 y^(2j+1)/(2j+1). After N=32 terms its remainder is between 0 and 2 y^(2N+1)/[(2N+1)(1-y^2)]. Each retained term is floored at scale 2^80; adding N plus the ceiling of the scaled tail is a rigorous upper enclosure. Range reduction p=2^k t and the separately enclosed ln 2 gives a natural-log interval; multiplication by negative k reverses endpoint order. The base-two division takes extrema of all four rational corner quotients, then floors/ceilings at scale 2^60. Powers of two are evaluated exactly. Multiplying by the negative entropy coefficient -p swaps endpoints. Linear interval arithmetic then encloses D with no floating sign decision.

The initial intervals certified 20136 cases nonnegative, including 504 exact zeros. The other 600 intervals contained zero. A separate frozen follow-up expands each D as a rational linear combination of logarithms of rational masses, factors numerator and denominator integers into primes, and sums each prime's exact coefficient. Every coefficient is zero in all 600 cases, proving D=0 without numerical precision refinement. The follow-up also matches each such parent spectrum using a second algorithm: recursively transform physical value pairs (u,v) to ((1-p)u+pv,v-u), then square the centered-basis coefficients and multiply by product variances. This is a second implementation by the same contributor, not independent review.

Consequently every representative satisfies D>=0; the proved invariances extend this finite certificate to the raw grid. There is no unsupported line within the stated finite claim, subject to independent implementation review. The first unsupported extrapolation would be: grid verification implies the same bound for all real biases and dimensions. We do not make it.

# A071 / B15 stage 2: exact five-bit erasure selection constant

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Following B15 STREAM_NOTES v2, decide E*(5)=max over all nonconstant Boolean signs f on the uniform five-bit cube of min over relevant physical coordinates i of B_i/Inf_i, where B_i=H(S_i|S_-i), P(S)=fhat(S)^2, and entropy is in bits. This is an actual-Boolean optimization, not a relaxation of spectral phases. The answer is

    K5 = 16/3 - (3/8)log2(3) - (169/24)log2(13) + (89/24)log2(89)
       = 2.695887520153101... .

Exactly 640 truth tables attain the maximum: their minority output class is {t, t xor e_i, t xor e_j} for distinct i,j, with either output sign. The representative negative mask is 7, i.e. {-1 points}= {0,1,2}, assignment order little-endian. This is the same cube-isometry orbit as the lead's f_5 mask 4194372. This stage decides the FULL five-bit domain, including minority sizes 7..16; it does not infer that result from the preliminary sparse census.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

Read BOARD.md snapshot 2026-10-11 23:39 UTC, B15 STREAM_NOTES.md v2 (2026-10-11 23:25 UTC), own STATUS/LEARNED files, and reread EXECUTION_PROTOCOL.json revision 2026-10-11-execution-v2. Read the B15 lead run's CLAIMS.json, specifically B15-LKSTAR-FAILS-N5-N6@1 and B15-SELECTION-EQUIVALENCE-COMPOSITION-INVARIANCE@1, and code/certify_witnesses.py to pin sign/table conventions. Consulted A075/A085 current status to avoid duplicating their assigned asymptotic and review work. Board records show the prior A071 B09 grid has scoped review support; we did not repeat it.

The lead's direction replaces our old sharper-biased-tax next action. The <=6 sparse domain by itself could not prove the requested full E*(5). We first stated that gap explicitly, then froze and executed an all-function census using covering cube symmetries. Local source byte hashes are saved in out/source_receipts.json. The new upper certificate does not assume either lead claim. C005 is used only to explain the conditional universal bridge, not to compute E*(5).

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

1. Write f=-1 exactly on A and +1 elsewhere, with N=32 assignments. Output complement permits 1<=|A|<=16. Translation x->x xor t multiplies each Fourier coefficient by a sign, leaving every squared coefficient, B_i and Inf_i unchanged; translating any point of A to zero anchors the mask at assignment 0. Coordinate permutations permute the five ratios, leaving their minimum unchanged. Therefore every nonconstant truth table has a representative with an odd negative mask, popcount <=16, influences sorted nondecreasingly, and mask minimal among all its translations that contain zero. These are COVERING filters, not unique orbit labels. Sorted influences stay sorted under translation. General affine coordinate mixing is not used because it changes physical influences.

2. All 2^31 odd masks from 1 to 2^32-1 were scanned. Exactly sum_{k=1}^{16} C(31,k-1)=2^30 are anchored minority masks. Of these, 80198472 have sorted physical influences and 5778648 survive anchored-translation minimality. Every surviving table is explicitly built as +/-1 and transformed by an integer batched FWHT. Let c_s=W_s/2; then p_s=c_s^2/256, and the code checks sum_s c_s^2=256. For a spectral pair s,s+i let a=c_s^2, b=c_{s+i}^2. Set

    G(a,b)=(a+b)log2(a+b)-a log2(a)-b log2(b),
    L_i=sum_{s not containing i} c_{s+i}^2.

Then B_i=sum_pairs G(a,b)/256 and Inf_i=L_i/256, so B_i/Inf_i=sum_pairs G(a,b)/L_i. Zero masses use 0 log 0=0; G(a,0)=G(0,b)=0. Independently, the number e_i of undirected physical edges on which f changes gives Inf_i=e_i/16. The full census checks L_i=16 e_i for every evaluated table and coordinate. Irrelevant coordinates have L_i=0 and are omitted.

3. Packet entropy bounds are rigorous. For integer z, range-reduce z=2^k t with 1<=t<2, put y=(t-1)/(t+1) in [0,1/3], and use

    ln t = 2 sum_{j=0}^{47} y^(2j+1)/(2j+1) + R,
    0<=R<=2 y^97/[97(1-y^2)].

Each term is floored at natural-log scale 2^128; adding 48 rounding units and the rounded-up tail gives the upper enclosure. The separately enclosed positive ln2 converts to log2; all integer logarithms here are nonnegative, so division uses lower numerator/upper denominator and upper numerator/lower denominator, with outward rounding to 96 bits. Powers of two are exact. In G, the positive coefficient uses its upper logarithm and the negative coefficients use lower logarithms for an upper bound. The already rigorous G interval is then outward rounded to 40 bits for the NumPy lookup table. Integer quotient ceiling gives a rigorous upper bound on each relevant ratio, and its minimum is an upper bound on E(f).

4. The sparse search first constructed a rigorous 96-bit interval and prime-log expression for the seed's exact minimum. The full census fixed a 40-bit lower integer bound on that value, seed_lower_scaled=2964159675584 at scale 2^40. For 5778647 representatives a relevant coordinate's computed upper ratio is <= that lower bound, which proves E(f)<=K5. Only one representative, mask 7, remained. A separate Python integer FWHT reconstructs that table, checks Parseval, its sign-valued inverse at all 32 physical assignments, and influence by direct edges. Exact prime factorization of G's logarithm arguments gives its minimizing ratio vector {2:16/3, 3:-3/8, 13:-169/24, 89:89/24}. The remaining relevant ratios are rigorously at least this value. Thus the seed attains K5, and the all-function covering proves E*(5)=K5.

5. Every other surviving representative is strictly below the seed, since the 40-bit seed lower bound is below the exact seed value. Hence any extremizer must map to mask 7 under output complement, translation and coordinate permutation. Exhaustive generation of that cube-isometry orbit has 320 negative support sets; their 320 complements are distinct, giving exactly 640 extremizing truth tables. Equivalently, choose t in 32 ways and the two distinct coordinate directions in C(5,2)=10 ways; the degree-two vertex of the three-point path identifies t uniquely. Both output signs give 640.

The proof is complete within dimension five, pending independent execution/review. The first unsupported extension would be replacing E*(5) by a bound on sup_n E*(n). In fact the lead's existing six-bit witness has E>368/125>K5, so K5 itself is already excluded as a universal selection constant. No finite census resolves the existence of some larger dimension-independent constant.

# A071 stage 3: exact six-bit-gate admission countersearch

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Let h be the six-bit Boolean sign negative exactly on {0,1,2,4,8,16,32,6,17,40}. For an actual Boolean core g on a uniform physical cube, form F=h(g_1,...,g_6) with six disjoint identical copies. Set B_i(g)=H(S_i|S without i), E(g)=min over relevant i of B_i(g)/Inf_i(g); all entropies are bits. Test the proposed admission helper that the signed empty-sector reserve is nonnegative, and its repairs by minority orientation or choosing either output sign. These are explicit A071 test hypotheses, not statements attributed to A075 or A056. The broader target is A075's arbitrary-core biased admission NEXT after its matching/path mechanism closure. No claim about universal FEI follows from a bounded erasure counterexample.

The result is an exact obstruction: for the six-bit one-point defect g, E(g)>9/4 but BOTH h(g^6) and h((-g)^6) have E<9/4. Thus an unguarded fixed-threshold admission theorem, even with a choice of output sign, is false. Separately the actual 36-bit h(h^6) has E between 323/100 and 324/100, excluding any universal selectable-erasure constant at most 323/100. The one-point obstruction has low E and does not refute an admission theorem restricted to E(g)>=K6 or to a larger hard-core threshold.

## sources actually read, revisions and theorem locators

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Write p=P(g=-1), m=1-2p, v=1-m^2, beta_i=ghat({i}), t=beta_i^2. Constants are handled by deletion. Expand h under independent biased inputs, in their normalized centered product basis, with coefficients b_T. For J excluding outer coordinate 1, put U_J=b_J^2 and V_J=b_(J union {1})^2. Define

    e(x,y)=(x+y)log2(x+y)-x log2 x-y log2 y,
    q(p)=2p(1-p)^3(2-p),
    M_p(t)=sum_J e(U_J, t V_J/v),
    R_p(t)=M_p(t)-q(p)e(m^2,t).

Zero terms use continuous endpoint values. Exact coefficient expansion, homogeneity of e and Parseval on the other occupied blocks give, for every physical coordinate in every copy,

    Inf_i(F)=q(p) Inf_i(g),
    B_i(F)=q(p) B_i(g)+R_p(beta_i^2).                 (1)

For the empty selected-child residual Fourier set the no-i amplitude is the recentered outer coefficient; its contribution is M_p. Nonempty selected-child residual sets contribute q times their child pairs. Removing precisely the child's empty pair produces (1). This is A056's recentered identity with an explicitly specialized gate, not an assumption that squared signed terms add incoherently. Independently, the gate minority probability is alpha(p)=(1-p)^4(1+4p-2p^2), and sum_J V_J=vq; both were verified exactly at every tested bias.

The core spectrum for g negative only at zero on six bits is ghat(empty)=31/32 and ghat(S)=-1/32 for every nonempty S. Every physical influence is 1/32. Hence all six core ratios equal

    E(g)=[62+962 log2(962)-961 log2(961)]/32
        =2.2922449382251937... >9/4.

Both g and -g are invariant under all six input permutations. The outer gate is transitive under swaps within its matched pairs and permutations of those pairs. Independent core permutations within each block and these outer automorphisms act transitively on all 36 physical coordinates, so a single computed ratio is the actual minimum, with no omitted cheap coordinate. Exact rational enclosing intervals and all 32 rational outer packets for each sign are in out/orientation_certificate.json. They certify:

    p=1/64: R=-0.0002579291303..., E(h(g^6))=2.1527061725... <9/4;
    p=63/64: R=-0.00000006946869..., E(h((-g)^6))=2.0008009627... <9/4.

All strict comparisons use rational interval endpoints, not these displayed decimals. The independent reconstruction directly sums the two signed conditional sections of h on its other five inputs, rather than reusing the centered-transform algorithm.

A smaller explicit physical-table control is g on three bits negative at all points except 7 (mask127): E(g)=2.6724889839... >8/3, while all 18 coordinates of h(g^6) have E=2.0761370119... <21/10. Its full actual truth table, integer Walsh transform, pair spectra and physical edge counts were reconstructed independently. This validates (1) against an explicit composition rather than only a compressed calculation.

The positive calibration h(h^6), with core p=5/32 and beta_i=-3/16, has q=5806485/16777216, every physical influence 17419455/268435456, reserve 0.01854441296..., and E=3.230266468892202... . Its rational lower endpoint is strictly greater than 323/100. The opposite orientation h((-h)^6) has E=2.63789894979... . Thus output complementation preserves the core's H,I,B,E but cannot be quotiented before applying a fixed biased outer gate.

The second family is complete in an important limited sense. For any six-bit Boolean core with M negative points and a chosen coordinate, let M0,M1 be negative counts in its two halves. Then p=M/64 and |beta|=|M0-M1|/32. Consequently d=|M0-M1| satisfies 0<=d<=min(M,64-M) and d congruent M modulo2. Conversely every such pair is realized by placing (M+d)/2 lex-first negatives in the coordinate-zero half and (M-d)/2 in the coordinate-one half. Since R depends only on (p,beta^2), the 559 signatures settle the reserve sign for every chosen coordinate of every six-bit Boolean core without enumerating 2^64 tables. They do not settle E minima or the admission implication for all cores. Among the 288 signatures with p<=1/2, 39 have negative reserve. Both output orientations are negative at exactly the four minority signatures (M,d)=(1,1),(2,2),(3,1),(3,3) in this six-bit projection.

The honest first unsupported line is now narrower: for an arbitrary core whose E already exceeds the campaign's hard-core threshold (at least K6), does some legitimate orientation/representation preserve that threshold after the signed reserves in (1), all physical coordinates, and actual terminal H(g) are retained? The low-E point defect does not answer this. Nor does E(h(h^6))>3.23 establish unbounded E or unbounded H/I. No recursion, asymptotic admission theorem, or terminal entropy payment is proved here.

For the logarithm certificates, write n=2^k r, 1<=r<2. For z=(r-1)/(r+1), 0<=z<1/3, use ln r=2 sum_(j>=0) z^(2j+1)/(2j+1). Floor each of 48 terms at scale 2^128; the upper error is 48 units plus the ceiling of the geometric tail 2 z^97/[97(1-z^2)]. Enclose ln2 by the same construction with z=1/3. Add k ln2 with positive k, divide positive interval endpoints outward, then round outward at 2^96. Powers of two are exact. Rational e(x,y) is evaluated after clearing a common denominator, with negative logarithmic coefficients rounded in the opposite direction. No floating value selects a sign, minimum, or threshold.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

PROOF_REVIEW.md reconstructs the full chain, including both real-parameter endpoint arguments, a different compact interval implementation, physical coefficients/edges, the infinite-family-to-finite-word telescope, the rare budget and the existing fixed-seed corollary. This is a mathematical review, not acceptance based on finite passes alone.

An independent chain-rule information proof gives ordinary output entropy h2(p')<=Q h2(p). Each chain-rule information term I(Y;X_j|X_<j) is at most I(Y;X_j|X_-j)=q_j h2(p), because the inputs are independent. Thus D=Q h2(p)-h2(p')>=0 and

    Psi(g)-Psi(g')=D/(Q I(g)).

The actual hierarchical spectral law gives H(g')=Q H(g)+C and I(g')=Q I(g), with C=H_p(gate)-Q h2(v), v=4p(1-p). This retains the entire starting core entropy. Spectral subadditivity gives C<=v sum_j h2(q_j). Actual raw biased derivative coefficients have absolute value<=1, giving C>=-Q v log2(e). No global coordinate transitivity is assumed for J.

For h at every real0<p<=1/16, direct probability/pivotal polynomials and elementary entropy bounds prove D>(105/4)p² and -144p²<=C<=360p², hence |C|/D<14. For J on that whole band, the signed Russo derivative proves D>(3/4)p; all six separate pivotal statuses obey min(q_j,1-q_j)<=5p, giving C/D<40 and -C/D<48. These are analytic endpoint proofs, not dyadic-grid extrapolations.

Our compact certificate replaces source Bernstein ranges by independently derived positive factor ranges on the same256 closed boxes. Every computed envelope-to-deficit quotient is below20 (maximum about19.41842713345), hence below48. Together the endpoints and compact coverage prove |C|<=48D for every permitted gate/bias, independently of core dimension. Each normalized signed entropy increment is therefore paid by48 times its own nonnegative Psi decrease; one telescope pays EVERY finite legal word once. Edge isoperimetry gives Psi<=11/16 on the rare band, so48Psi<=33.

The exact retained seed calculation gives H(g3)/I(g3)+48Psi(g3)=9.9481922336...<10 using rational interval endpoints. This establishes the all-word10 bound for that particular fixed seed. A071 previously supplied the one-flip bias-map target; that is acknowledged provenance, not a substantive repair of A075's new output-potential proof. The original64 theorem is accepted independently at its unchanged statement and hypotheses; the separate48 corollary is a contributed quantitative refinement with review pending.

The first unsupported universal line is an actual signed-row/child-surplus payment for the retained arbitrary starting H(g), beyond singleton caps. A useful exact scope diagnostic is

    I(g)-h2(p)=H(X|g(X))-sum_i H(X_i|g(X),X_-i),

for independent uniform physical X. Its nonnegativity supplies no comparison with Fourier entropy. On the rare band a finite bound H(g)<=K[I(g)-h2(p)] would already be FEI-equivalent up to constants, since h2(p)<=11I/16 there. Positivity of this reserve is therefore not declared a solution to its unpaid entropy bill.

## exact target, quantifiers, hypotheses and units

Complete packet A072's baseline algebraic engine obligation for the actual family frozen by A071, targeting B09-BIASED-BLOCK-UNCERTAINTY@1. For all 128 output-complement representatives on three bits, every p in {1/8,1/4,1/2}^3, and each physical block mask 1..6, verify the signed spectrum, Parseval, exact Boolean inverse, physical edge influence, child-mixture marginal, and retained-coordinate influence decomposition. No entropy comparison is performed or claimed.

Assignments x=0..2^n-1 use little-endian bits. The uniform integer Walsh character is (-1)^popcount(x&s), corresponding to cube coordinates 1-2*x_i. The integer transform W has coefficient denominator N=2^n and probability denominator N^2. The biased orthonormal character uses (x_i-p_i)/sqrt(p_i(1-p_i)), corresponding at p=1/2 to the opposite one-bit orientation. Its coefficient differs from W_s/N by (-1)^|s| at uniform bias; squared masses agree. All spectral and pivotal quantities are dimensionless rational probabilities; downstream entropy is in bits. The API separately returns pivotal probabilities and biased spectral influences, rather than conflating their normalizations.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Let N=2^n and W_u=sum_x f(x)(-1)^popcount(x&u). Orthogonality follows by pairing x with x xor 2^i whenever the character difference contains i. Consequently WHT(W)=N*f, sum_u W_u^2=N^2 for sign f, and the inverse has exactly the input signs. The reused butterfly computes the same character sums; the checker independently compares it with direct character enumeration for every uniform table. If e_i counts each disagreeing undirected i-edge once, the physical influence is 2*e_i/N. Fourier expansion of (f(x)-f(x xor 2^i))/2 retains exactly the characters containing i; Parseval gives Inf_i=sum_{s contains i}W_s^2/N^2. Both sides are computed independently.

For rational interior biases define v_i=p_i(1-p_i). Expanding 1-2*x_i=(1-2*p_i)-2*(x_i-p_i) in the uniform expansion gives the radical-free centered coefficient

    b_s = (-2)^|s| sum_{u superset s} (W_u/N) product_{i in u\s}(1-2*p_i).

Thus f(x)=sum_s b_s product_{i in s}(x_i-p_i), the orthonormal signed coefficient is b_s*sqrt(product_{i in s}v_i), and its squared mass is b_s^2*product_{i in s}v_i. This polynomial expansion follows directly from the finite Walsh inverse and holds for every n and rational interior bias; its general correctness is an elementary identity, not an all-dimensional entropy theorem. The implementation checks all physical inverse evaluations in Fraction arithmetic and their exact membership in {-1,+1}, including every distinct child table. The centered products are orthogonal by independence and centering, so Parseval gives total spectral mass one, also checked exactly.

For coordinate i let Q_i=P_{X_-i}[f(0,X_-i)!=f(1,X_-i)]. The difference d_i=f(1,X_-i)-f(0,X_-i) equals sum_{s contains i}b_s product_{j in s\{i}}(X_j-p_j). Its squared expectation is 4Q_i for sign f. Orthogonality therefore gives

    sum_{s contains i}mu(s) = v_i E[d_i^2] = 4p_i(1-p_i)Q_i.

The engine independently sums the physical edge probabilities over X_-i and compares this result with the spectral marginal. At p_i=1/2 it coincides with ordinary cube influence. All total influences are the sums of their saved coordinate vectors. No biased Fourier coefficient is mislabeled as an integer Walsh coefficient.

For any physical block A, expanding the partial coefficient on B in the centered A-products gives q(t)=sum_{r subset A}mu(r union t)=sum_z P_A(z)mu_z(t). This is orthogonality on A, with the physical branches and inherited biases. All 62208 marginal entries are independently checked via constructed child tables. Averaging the edge pivotal event over the physical assignments in A gives E_z J_i(f_z)=J_i(f) for i in B, where J_i=4p_i(1-p_i)Q_i. This retained-coordinate influence identity is also checked for every block. It is the algebraic input to the nonreusing physical influence ledger; it does not pay the entropy residual.

The A071 spectrum implementation evaluates weighted centered moments directly. Our conversion expands uniform Walsh polynomials and uses physical edge counts. The two methods give exactly identical masses on 3612 distinct parent/child inputs. Extracting only A071's spectrum function for comparison executes no A071 search or logarithm code. This is independent algebraic reproduction by A072; it is not an independent review of A071's logarithm bounds, zero-factor certificates or final entropy verdict.

Named calibration: AND with a dummy third bit and p=(1/8,1/4,1/2) has spectrum (225,7,3,21,0,0,0,0)/256; Q=(1/4,1/8,0); spectral influences (7/64,3/32,0), total 13/64. The A071 minimum-positive witness is +1 except x=3 at p=(1/8,1/8,1/2), block A={2}. Its integer W=(6,2,2,-2,-2,2,2,-2); biased masses (3969,7,7,49,1,7,7,49)/4096; Q=(1/16,1/16,1/64); spectral influences (7/256,7/256,1/64), total 9/128. The two children have spectra (961,7,7,49)/1024 and (1,0,0,0), each with physical weight 1/2. These records allow certified entropy reproduction without guessing phases or branch weights.

No unsupported line is used in the algebraic certificate. The first unsupported global step would be to conclude a dimension-independent entropy payment, a sharper valid endpoint tax, or full FEI from these finite checks. Those implications are not made.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

First run the prior uniform certificate on every parent and physical child: integer WHT; WHT(W)=Nf; sum W_s^2=N^2; direct character sums agree; physical undirected edge counts give Inf_i=2e_i/N=sum_(s containing i)mu(s). These checks precede entropy expressions. The exact masks reconstruct the actual Boolean functions, rather than assuming a proposed spectral law is Boolean.

For a packet indexed by s not containing i let a=mu(s), b=mu(s union i), q=a+b. Define phi(a,b)=q log2 q-a log2 a-b log2 b, with zero terms omitted. Then B_i=sum_s phi(a,b)=H(S_i|S_-i). Physical children are independently made from the truth table by setting x_i=0 or 1; their spectra are certified as above. Their signed Walsh coefficients satisfy 2W_child,0=u+v and 2W_child,1=u-v for parent packet values u,v. Consequently (mu_0+mu_1)/2=q exactly, also checked by Fractions. Define JS_i=H(q)-(H(mu_0)+H(mu_1))/2 and Delta_iH=H(mu)-(H(mu_0)+H(mu_1))/2. Since H(mu)=H(q)+B_i, Delta_iH=B_i+JS_i, verified by exact prime-log coefficient equality for all 11 witness coordinates (and all 32 pilot coordinates). The average physical-child influence vector equals the parent's retained-coordinate influence vector exactly. This is the conserved physical ledger, not an entropy-payment bound.

For every positive rational t, factor numerator and denominator and record log2 t=sum_p valuation_p(t) log2 p. Every entropy and erasure expression is therefore a finite rational coefficient map. The zero term t log t is omitted. Exact equality of maps certifies equality of the expressions. Nonzero prime-log forms cannot cancel: clearing denominators gives a product of prime powers; unique factorization detects exact zero. To compare two maps, clear rational denominators D and compare A=product_(e_p>0)p^e_p with B=product_(e_p<0)p^(-e_p). The sign of the original form is the sign of A-B because the logarithm is strictly increasing and D>0. All products are Python arbitrary-precision integers. The checker uses the integer bound sum_p |e_p| bit_length(p) before construction and refuses above the frozen 8000000-bit budget. It stores the exponents, denominator and product bit sizes; recomputation requires no numerical logarithm.

The saved minimum_comparisons compare every relevant coordinate's ratio to the selected minimum; every sign is nonnegative and exactly zero identifies the listed minimizing coordinates. ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

### Signed reserve and physical influence, with an independent engine

Write p=P(g=-1), m=1-2p, v=1-m^2, beta_i=ghat({i}), t=beta_i^2. Expand the outer sign as h(y)=A(y_-j)+y_j D(y_-j), where y_j=+1 corresponds to physical bit x_j=0. For T on the other five outputs take normalized product Fourier coefficients a_T,d_T; set C_T=a_T+m d_T, U_T=C_T^2, D_T=d_T^2 and q=sum_T D_T. Exact boundary enumeration gives q=2p(1-p)^3(2-p)>0. Define phi(x,y)=(x+y)log2(x+y)-x log2 x-y log2 y and

    R_p(t)=sum_T phi(U_T,t D_T)-q phi(m^2,t).

For a nonempty selected-child residual spectral set, the coordinate-pair amplitudes both have multiplier d_T times the normalized nonempty spectra of occupied other blocks. Homogeneity of phi and Parseval on those blocks give q times the corresponding child's pair contribution. For the selected empty residual, the no-i amplitude is C_T and the with-i amplitude is beta_i d_T. Consequently, including the child's removed empty pair precisely once,

    B_(j,i)(F)=q B_i(g)+R_p(beta_i^2),
    Inf_(j,i)(F)=q Inf_i(g).                                 (1)

The influence equality also follows directly: a physical edge can change F iff it changes g_j and the outer gate is pivotal given the other five block outputs. Outer matching-pair permutations and swaps act transitively on all six j, so the same reserve function applies in every block. No square of a signed sum is replaced by an incoherent mixture. Constants are excluded; irrelevant core coordinates have beta=0, B=0 and no influence, so none become newly relevant.

The new independent path directly sums signed conditional sections h0,h1 over the 32 other-output assignments. For each T it computes expectation of centered-bit products times (h0+h1)/2+m(h0-h1)/2 and (h0-h1)/2. Dividing their squared moments by [p(1-p)]^|T| gives U_T,D_T. A second, reused checked uniform-Walsh-to-biased polynomial conversion on the actual outer truth table verifies these exact masses, sign inverse, Parseval and all physical pivotal probabilities at every one of 63 biases. Those checks precede the reserve comparisons. out/outer_bias_M.json saves all 32 rational U,D packets, all 64 outer spectral masses and the physical multiplier q for every M.

### Complete signature domain and one orientation for every coordinate

Let M be the number of negative points of a six-bit core. For a chosen coordinate let M0,M1 be its half counts, d=|M0-M1|. Necessarily

    1<=M<=63, 0<=d<=min(M,64-M), d congruent M (mod 2),
    p=M/64, |beta|=d/32.                                    (2)

Conversely place (M+d)/2 lex-first negatives in its zero half and (M-d)/2 in its one half. This constructs an actual sign core realizing every signature. Each witness is checked with integer Walsh, exact sign inverse, Parseval and independent physical edges. There are exactly 559 signatures, all reconstructed. The sign counts are 440 positive,31 zero,88 negative; every one agrees with A071's independently saved result. Zero cases have d=0 and exact R=0. Fraction/outward logarithm intervals decide every other sign without tolerance.

For each minority count M=4..32, at least one orientation has R>=0 for EVERY feasible d simultaneously. The common choice is p=M/64 except choose p=1-M/64 for M=16..20. The exact 29-row policy, including all alternative safe signs, is saved in out/orientation_policy.json. Since this policy is uniform across d, it applies to all coordinates of one actual core using a single sign, rather than selecting a different sign per coordinate. Combining this with (1) gives the desired ratio inequality as soon as M>=4.

### Exclude all sparse bad sectors under the high-E hypothesis

Output complement preserves B,I and E, so take the smaller negative side. If it has at most three points, physical input translation normalizes one point to zero. Singleton support has one orbit. Two points are classified by their Hamming distance 1..6, yielding six orbits. For three points {0,u,w}, each nonconstant coordinate column belongs to one of the three patterns (10),(01),(11). Let their counts be a,b,c. Coordinate permutations reorder columns; changing the anchored point and relabeling the unordered triple permutes the three counts. Thus sorting 0<=a<=b<=c, with a+b>0 and a+b+c<=6, is a COMPLETE cube-isometry covering. The remaining columns are dummy coordinates. This gives sixteen triple representatives. No general affine transformation, which could change physical influence, is used.

The total 1+6+16=23 representatives is not a sampled sparse search. For every representative the prior checked Walsh/erasure engine constructs its six-bit truth table, every physical child, exact B_i/JS_i/decrement, inverse and edges. It finds a relevant coordinate with B_i/Inf_i<14/5; each strict inequality is certified by its exact rational prime-log form and a denominator-cleared integer prime-power comparison. These 23 certificates cover every actual six-bit core with at most three minority points. Hence E(g)>=14/5 excludes M<=3, and the common-orientation policy proves the finite guarded theorem. The integer certificate out/guard_gap.json separately proves K6>14/5, where K6=16/3-(21/8)log2 3+(95/24)log2 5-(121/24)log2 11+(65/24)log2 13. A075's all-dimensional matching theorem is not a premise.

### Full terminal entropy is retained

Let pi_p(T) be the exact normalized biased outer spectrum, and let rho(S)=ghat(S)^2/v for nonempty S, the centered core spectral law. Each occupied outer block is filled by a nonempty inner spectral set; the occupied-block pattern and actual nonempty inner sets determine the physical set uniquely. Therefore the Fourier law is a disjoint hierarchical law and

    H(F)=H_p(h)+sum_j [v q] H(rho)
        =6q H(g)+C_p,
    C_p=H_p(h)-6q h2(v),
    I(F)=6q I(g).                                            (3)

To verify the second equality, v H(rho)=H(g)+m^2 log2(m^2)+v log2 v; zero conventions cover m=0. No terminal H(g) is discarded. Every out/outer_bias_M.json saves the exact rational-log terms for C_p and its signed coefficients. The guarded E theorem neither bounds C_p by physical influence nor pays a whole normalized reference or a stopping tree. That remains a distinct obligation, including after physical restrictions change block biases.

The engine checks equality of every core coordinate's exact B/I form before using that argument. The point defect has E>9/4 but both compositions have E<9/4 and negative reserve. The positive fixture satisfies 323/100<E(h(h^6))<324/100; the complemented seed composition has E<8/3. out/fixtures.json contains exact outward bounds and core erasure forms. ### Arithmetic enclosure and first unsupported line

For integer n>0 write n=2^k r with 1<=r<2. Put z=(r-1)/(r+1)<1/3. The positive series ln r=2 sum_(j>=0)z^(2j+1)/(2j+1), after 48 terms, has remainder between zero and 2z^97/[97(1-z^2)]. Each term is floored at scale 2^128; add 48 units plus the ceiling of that scaled remainder to obtain an upper bound. Independently enclose ln2 by z=1/3, divide nonnegative endpoints outward at scale 2^96, and add k exactly after division. Powers of two are exact. Homogeneity cancels the common denominator in phi(x,y), leaving a linear combination of positive integer logarithms; negative coefficients use opposite endpoints. This independently ranged enclosure differs from A071's correlated k ln2 enclosure. For sparse rational prime-log forms, clear coefficient denominators and compare arbitrary-precision integer products; a preconstruction 8000000-bit bound enforces the resource limit. No floating entropy chooses a sign or coordinate.

The first unsupported universal line is that the safe orientation policy holds for all continuous feasible p,t arising from arbitrary-dimensional hard cores, or that every remaining bad signature can be excluded by E>=14/5 in all dimensions. The six-bit signature grid and 23 sparse isometry classes do not prove those lines. Applying the six-bit admission again to a 36-bit composed core is unsupported, even if its E exceeds 3.23. No unbounded E or H/I family, global C018 bound or terminal C019 payment is asserted.

    M(F)=qmM(f)+k[q(1-q)+q^2 log2(d/q^2)],

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Every binary truth table has the unique multilinear interpolation f(x)=sum_T c_T prod_{i∈T}x_i. Subset Möbius inversion gives c_T=sum_{U⊆T}(-1)^{|T\U|}f(1_U). Successive differences compute these integer c_T, independently of Walsh transforms or weighted centered moments. Evaluating the polynomial at every binary input reproduces the original table exactly.

Set u_i=x_i-p_i. Expanding x_i=p_i+u_i gives b_S=sum_{T⊇S}c_T prod_{i∈T\S}p_i. Thus f=sum_S b_S prod_{i∈S}u_i and μ(S)=b_S² prod_{i∈S}p_i(1-p_i). Independence and centering prove orthogonality. We check every inverse evaluation and sum μ=1. Separately count physical disagreeing edges with product probabilities Q_i over other coordinates. Since f(1,x_-i)-f(0,x_-i) is 0 or ±2, orthogonality gives sum_{S∋i}μ(S)=4p_i(1-p_i)Q_i. We reconcile both computations; these quantities must not be confused away from uniform bias.

For each physical assignment z to A, construct its truth table afresh and apply the same polynomial routine on B=Aᶜ. Let w_z be its physical probability, μ_z its spectrum, q_t=sum_{S:S∩B=t}μ(S), and r(z,t)=w_z μ_z(t). Partial orthogonality gives q_t=sum_z r(z,t); this is checked exactly. The retained-coordinate spectral influence equals its w_z-weighted child influence, also checked.

Compute the quantities directly from the two conditional experiments:

    M=-sum_S μ(S) log2(μ(S)/q_(S∩B)),
    J=sum_(z,t) w_z μ_z(t) log2(μ_z(t)/q_t).

Zero masses are omitted. This avoids the previous parent/child entropy-combination and prime-product representations. It retains both the spectral selector and physical assignment, rather than identifying these different random variables.

Logarithms use a third representation. For 1≤x≤2 put t=1-1/x∈[0,1/2]. Integrating the geometric series yields ln x=sum_{k≥1}t^k/k. Its 40-term partial sum a obeys a≤ln x≤a+t^41/[41(1-t)], since each remaining denominator is at least41. For any positive rational r, find integer k with r=2^k x,1≤x<2; enclose log2 r=k+ln x/ln2. Both numerator bounds are nonnegative and the ln2 denominator interval positive, so division is lower/upper reversed in the denominator. Negative entropy weights reverse interval endpoints. All these operations use integers and Fraction, with no transcendental floating arithmetic.

First witness reproduces μ=(3969,7,7,49,1,7,7,49)/4096, q=(1985,7,7,49)/2048 and equally weighted children (961,7,7,49)/1024 and (1,0,0,0). The fresh rigorous interval is strictly inside (0.00256647,0.00256648); consequently D>5/2048. This name denotes the previously selected record, not an independently established global minimum.

Second witness is g(x_0,x_1)(1-2x_2). Its spectrum has deterministic selector membership and child laws both equal q=(289,343,343,49)/1024. Therefore M=J=D=0 exactly, with no approximate equality test.

AND reproduces μ=(225,7,3,21)/256, q=(29,3)/32, children (1,0) with weight7/8 and (1/4,3/4) with weight1/8. Direct channel/conditional calculations give 0.11954501<R<0.11954502, hence R>1195/10000. The tax-free arbitrary-product-bias helper is false at this scope. Full FEI is not refuted.

No differing rational quantity or unsupported line remains in these fixed calculations. The first unsupported extrapolation would be accepting every A071 grid case, its global minimum, an all-bias theorem, or dimension-independent nonreusing influence payment. We make none of those extrapolations.

## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

Let N=2^n and A be the negative physical assignments. For every nonzero character s, sum_x χ_s(x)=0 by pairing an input with a flip in s. Hence

    W_0=N-2|A|,
    W_s=-2(|A|-2|{x∈A:popcount(x&s) odd}|), s≠0.

We implement these direct character sums by intersections with precomputed character-sign bitsets and arbitrary-precision Python bit_count. There is no butterfly transform, incremental Gray transform or imported spectrum. Character orthogonality proves this is the uniform Fourier transform with denominator N. The census checks sum W_s²=N² for every table. Witnesses additionally check the complete signed inverse at each physical assignment. Physical undirected disagreeing i-edges e_i are counted independently, giving Inf_i=2e_i/N. Every census coordinate reconciles this with sum_(s∋i)W_s²/N².

For s not containing i let p0=μ(s),p1=μ(s∪{i}),q=p0+p1. These are the exact joint spectral law of (S_i,S_-i); when q>0 the conditional law is (p0/q,p1/q). Direct conditional entropy is

    B_i=-sum_(s not containing i) sum_(j=0,1) pj log2(pj/q).

Zero masses contribute zero. For both witnesses the script constructs every nonzero joint packet as Fractions and sums this expression. Separate 60-digit mpmath evaluation uses those same exact joint probabilities as numerical evidence only. Actual physical-child tables are independently restricted from the input masks, transformed by the direct character sums, and their average squared spectra are checked to equal q packet by packet.

Rational logarithm bounds use our previous stage's independent positive series, reconstructed here without importing that implementation. If1≤x≤2 and t=1-1/x, then ln x=sum_(k≥1)t^k/k; integrating the geometric series proves the identity. Its40-term partial sum a gives a≤ln x≤a+t^41/[41(1-t)]. Range-reduce any positive rational r=2^k x with1≤x<2; divide the nonnegative numerator interval by the positive independently bounded ln2 and add k. Signed entropy weights reverse endpoints when negative. No mpmath or machine logarithm decides a sign.

For the census, write a=W_s²,b=W_(s+i)². Its conditional packet numerator is G(a,b)=(a+b)log2(a+b)-a log2(a)-b log2(b). We outward-round each rational enclosure to scale2^64, caching the possible integer packet pairs. G(a,0)=0 is exact. Let L_i=sum_(s∋i)W_s². Integer comparison Kstar_lower_scaled*L_i>sum packet_upper_scaled proves B_i<Kstar Inf_i. Every relevant coordinate is inspected; any such strict witness is enough. Unresolved ties use exact prime-log cancellation for Kstar Inf_i-B_i: factor integer log arguments and cancel rational coefficients. In each equality case, every relevant coordinate's coefficient vector vanishes, proving all those ratios equal Kstar. Irrelevant coordinates are omitted.

All truth masks from0 through2^N-1 are covered without symmetry reduction. The strict/equality classifications are recorded per mask in JSONL. The equality masks are also compared as sets with all embedded three-bit point defects and their output complements. These are exactly16 tables at n3 and64 tables at n4. A three-bit point defect has μ_empty=9/16, seven other masses1/16, Inf_i=1/4. Its conditional entropy divided by influence simplifies to Kstar, independently proving sharpness; embedding dummy coordinates preserves this ratio. The exact integer inequality2^53*5^100<3^180 proves Kstar<107/40.

    minimizing coordinates={0,1,3}.
    E(f6)=(16/3)log2(2)-(21/8)log2(3)+(95/24)log2(5)-(121/24)log2(11)+(65/24)log2(13),
    minimizing coordinates={0,1,2,3,4,5}.

Our direct joint-law intervals prove every coordinate exceeds the prescribed rational cutoff and Kstar; symbolic equality of ratio forms identifies ties and disjoint rational intervals establish all remaining minimum comparisons. Full spectra, edges, influences, B prime forms and minimum forms match A072. Fresh intervals also prove E(f6)>E(f5). ## complete derivation or exact first unsupported line

**Independent representation.** Write t_i=(-1)^(1_(i∈S)). For each matched pair its contribution to the negative indicator's character sum is t_i+t_j+t_i t_j:3 once and−1 three times. An isolated coordinate contributes1 or−1 once each. The multiplicities of the sum z are coefficients of the Laurent generating polynomial

    (z^3+3 z^-1)^k (z+z^-1)^l.

Our code constructs these coefficients by repeated integer convolution, independently of A075's binomial (u,v) enumeration. The indicator sum at S is1+z. Nonempty Fourier coefficients are−2(1+z)/N with N=2^r. At the one empty index replace the formal−2a/N by1−2a/N, a=1+3k+l. The squared-Walsh histogram therefore removes exactly one4a² atom and inserts one(N−2a)² atom. Counts and Parseval are verified exactly.

To reconstruct a paired coordinate's conditional law, convolve the other k−1 pairs and all l isolates. If their sum is z, put u=1+z. The target pair contributes joint squared-Walsh packets(4(u+3)²,4(u−1)²) when its partner is absent and(4(u−1)²,4(u−1)²) when present. For an isolated direction, use the other k pairs and l−1 isolates and packet(4(u+1)²,4(u−1)²). The unique empty packet receives the same exceptional replacement. These are joint probabilities after division by N², not an arbitrary realizability relaxation. Erasure is summed directly as−p0 log2(p0/q)−p1 log2(p1/q), q=p0+p1. Our second representation visits the actual negative physical points and directly sums characters, without a Walsh butterfly. Signed spectra, packet multiplicities and physical edges reconcile for every directly checked root.

A paired direction has two internal negative edges and an isolated direction one. Hence crossing counts areδ=a−4 andδ=a−2 respectively, Inf_i=2δ/N and fhat({i})=−2δ/N. The checker separately counts disagreeing physical edges and reconciles spectral influence.

**Restriction closure.** If all fixed inputs are zero, intact pairs remain pairs and broken pairs leave isolates. If exactly one fixed input is one, the only possible negative free assignments are zero and its free partner's unit vector; the latter coordinate is a dummy of a point defect. Two fixed ones leave a point defect only when they form a matched pair, otherwise a constant; three or more fixed ones leave a constant. Degenerate full-negative small cubes are constants too. This exhausts simultaneous histories. Further point-defect restrictions retain point defects/dummies/constants. Our classifier predicts the negative residual set from this argument and compares it with direct conditioning of the original physical point set at every history through six bits.

**All-dimensional cutoff.** For any spectral law let q0=fhat(empty)² and qi=fhat({i})². Every ordinary erasure packet is at most its mass. The exceptional empty-remainder packet is at most qi log2(e(q0+qi)/qi), because x log2(1+y/x)≤y/ln2. Therefore

    B_i ≤ 1−q0−qi+qi log2(e/qi).

For this family qi=4δ²/N² and1−q0≤4a/N. Using1/ln2<3/2 (the positive atanh expansion of ln2 has first term2/3 and positive remainder) gives, for r>3,

    B_i/Inf_i ≤ U(r)=2+8/(r−3)+(3r+2)(2r+3/2)/2^r.

Hereδ≥r−3,a≤1+3r/2 and2a/δ≤2+8/(r−3). We verify U(13)=240651/81920<368/125<K6. The rational term decreases. For P(r)=(3r+2)(2r+3/2),

    2P(r)−P(r+1)=6r²−(7/2)r−23/2>0 for r≥13,

since it is positive at13 and has positive derivative thereon. Thus U decreases for every r≥13. The remaining r≤12 comprise49 parameter pairs including the zero-dimensional constant,76 relevant coordinate orbits. Exact rational intervals certify75 strict inequalities; the single equality orbit(k,l)=(3,0) represents all six coordinates and is checked by exact cancellation into the stated K6 prime-log expression. No overlapping numerical interval is used to assert equality.

Point-defect descendants on s non-dummy variables have d=2^(1−s) and

    B_i/d=2−2d+Phi((1−d)²,d²)/d
         ≤2+d(2s−4+log2 e).

At s=1 erasure is zero, at s=2 the bound is<11/4, and for s≥3 the correction2^(1−s)(2s−5/2) decreases from its value at3. This yields B_i/d<23/8<368/125<K6. Therefore the finite certificate plus analytic tail and closure prove the sharp bound at every descendant. Dummy directions are irrelevant; cube reflections/output sign preserve squared spectra and coordinate permutations merely relabel ratios. Equality is precisely the six-variable three-pair core up to these operations. This also proves sharpness for min_i B_i/Inf_i and aggregate B/I within the class.

**Stopping ledger, with terminals.** For any actual physical split, parent Fourier amplitudes u,v give child amplitudes u+v,u−v; averaging child squared laws gives q=u²+v². Entropy chain rules give Δ_iH=B_i+JS_i. In natural units the JS packet is q d(2uv/q), where d(z)=((1+z)ln(1+z)+(1−z)ln(1−z))/2. The bound d(z)≤1−sqrt(1−z²) follows from atanh(z)≤z/sqrt(1−z²): both vanish at0 and the derivative of the latter is(1−z²)^−3/2≥(1−z²)^−1, the derivative of atanh. Evenness handles negative z. Thus JS_i≤(2/ln2)sum min(u²,v²)≤(2/ln2)Inf_i. The ordinary influence identity is I=Inf_i+average I(children). Applying the hereditary bound and telescoping gives for every stopping tree

    H(f)≤E H(leaf)+(K6+2/ln2)[I(f)−E I(leaf)].

Our split checks reconcile signed child coefficients, mixed squared laws and retained physical influence at every actual node through six bits. The general argument retains both terminal quantities and spends each removed physical coordinate once. It does not assume zero-entropy leaves for an arbitrary stopping tree.

**Limits and adjacent mechanisms.** For perfect matchingsδ=a−4 and r=2k. The same scalar inequality proves x²+y²−Phi(x²,y²)≤(|x|−|y|)²/ln2. Partner-present packets have zero deficit; the other amplitude differences are at most8/N on at most N/4 rows. Consequently

    2a/δ−2(a²+δ²)/(δN)−8/(δ ln2)
       ≤E≤2a/δ+(2δ/N)(2r+3/2),

so E→2. With T=1−q0, every nonempty Fourier numerator is a nonzero integer of magnitude between2 and2a, and the empty entropy is at most T/ln2. Hence T(2r−2−2log2 a)≤H≤T(2r−2+1/ln2). Dividing by I=2rδ/N proves H/I→4. These are analytic limits, not finite trends.

For a graph-edge negative set,δ_i=a−2(1+degree_i) is again both the boundary count and singleton numerator parameter. A minimum-degree vertex hasδ≥a(1−4/n) for n>4; a≤(n²+n+2)/2. The same empty-packet bound gives the source's E≤2+8/(n−4)+(n²+n+2)(2n+3/2)/2^n. Its value at16 is551825/196608<368/125; monotonicity follows from2Q(n)−Q(n+1)=2n³−(5/2)n²−(15/2)n−8, positive and increasing for n≥16. A monotone path of length m has boundary m−1 in active directions and m+1 outside, and singleton parameter2i−m−1 in active direction i (one-based). If m<n choose an outside direction; if m=n choose a middle direction with singleton parameter of magnitude≤1. This yields precisely the source's two path bounds. At10 these are2521/1024 and22571/9216, both<368/125; the quadratic-tail difference4n²−n−8 and decreasing rational factors prove monotonicity thereafter. Smaller fixed dimensions have finite granularity bounds. Thus neither adjacent root mechanism can produce unbounded E. These graph/path root conclusions are not transferred to arbitrary descendants.

No unsupported line remains in A075's scoped claim. The first unsupported universal line is a uniformly paid signed collision/admission reserve for a non-sparse arbitrary inner Boolean core, at its actual bias and every newly relevant coordinate, with complete terminal entropy. The sparse-family theorem supplies none of that operation.
